\displaystyle=\exp\bigg\{\int_{t}^{s}\bigg[\tilde{b}_{1x}+g_{y}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}g_{z^{i}}\sigma_{ix}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}g_{\tilde{z}^{i}}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}f_{ix}\nu_{i}(de)

(2.117)

\displaystyle+\bigg(\tilde{b}_{1y}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\tilde{b}_{1z^{i}}\sigma_{ix}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\tilde{b}_{1\tilde{z}^{i}}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}f_{ix}\nu_{i}(de)\bigg)\hat{y}_{r}\bigg]dr\bigg\}.

Then we have

\hat{y}_{t}=E^{\hat{P}}\bigg[\Gamma_{t_{n}}\hat{y}_{t_{n}}+\int_{t}^{t_{n}}g_{x}\Gamma_{s}ds\bigg|\mathcal{F}_{t}\bigg].

(2.118)

Note that
|\hat{y}_{t_{n}}|=|\phi_{x}(x_{t_{n}})|\leq K_{0}
and combine with condition (
2.102
)

\displaystyle\tilde{b}_{1x}+g_{y}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}g_{z^{i}}\sigma_{ix}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}g_{\tilde{z}^{i}}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}f_{ix}\nu_{i}(de)+\bigg(\tilde{b}_{1y}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\tilde{b}_{1z^{i}}\sigma_{ix}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\tilde{b}_{1\tilde{z}^{i}}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}f_{ix}\nu_{i}(de)\bigg)\hat{y}_{r}

(2.119)

\displaystyle=\tilde{b}_{1x}+g_{y}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}g_{z^{i}}\sigma_{ix}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}g_{\tilde{z}^{i}}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}f_{ix}\nu_{i}(de)

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(2+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\nu_{i}(\mathcal{E}_{i})\bigg)K^{2}+2K=\hat{K},

thus we have
\Gamma_{s}\leq e^{\hat{K}(s-t)}
. Then,

\hat{y}_{t}\leq e^{\hat{K}(t_{n}-t)}K_{0}+\int_{t}^{t_{n}}Ke^{\hat{K}(s-t)}ds\leq e^{\hat{K}(t_{n}-t)}K_{0}+e^{\hat{K}(t_{n}-t)}-1=e^{\hat{K}(t_{n}-t)}(K_{0}+1)-1,

(2.120)

which implies that

|\theta_{x}(t,x)|\leq\tilde{K}:=e^{\hat{K}(t_{n}-t_{n-1})}(K_{0}+1)-1,\ t\in[t_{n-1},t_{n}].

(2.121)

Step 5.
We note that
\tilde{K}\leq\bar{K}
. Assume that
\theta
is defined on
[t_{k},t_{n}]
with
|\theta_{x}(t_{k},x)|\leq K_{k}\leq\bar{K}
. Define
\theta(t,x):=y^{t,x}_{t}
for
t\in[t_{k-1},t_{k}]
,
k=n-1,n-2,\cdots,1
, where

\left\{\begin{aligned} x^{t,x}_{s}&=x+\int_{t}^{s}\tilde{b}_{1}\big(r,\omega,\Theta^{t,x}(r)\big)dr+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{t}^{s}\sigma_{i}(r,\omega,x^{t,x}_{r})dW^{i}_{r}\\
&\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{t}^{s}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}f_{i}(r,\omega,x^{t,x}_{r-},e)\tilde{N}_{i}(de,dr),\\
y^{t,x}_{s}&=\theta(t_{k},\omega,x_{t_{k}}^{t,x})+\int_{s}^{t_{k}}g\big(r,\omega,\Theta^{t,x}(r)\big)dr-\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{s}^{t_{k}}z^{i,t,x}_{r}dW^{i}_{r}\\
&\quad-\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{s}^{t_{k}}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\tilde{z}^{i,t,x}_{(r,e)}\tilde{N}_{i}(de,dr),\end{aligned}\right.

(2.122)

by the analysis in
Step 4
, we can obtain

|\theta_{x}(t,x)|\leq K_{k-1}:=e^{\hat{K}(t_{k}-t_{k-1})}(K_{k}+1)-1,t\in[t_{k-1},t_{k}].

(2.123)

By induction, note that
K_{n}=K_{0}
, we get

K_{k}=e^{\hat{K}(t_{n}-t_{k})}(K_{0}+1)-1\leq\bar{K},\ k=1,\dots,n.

(2.124)

Therefore, the backward induction can continue until
k=1
, and thus we may define
\theta
on
[0,T]
and
|\theta_{x}(t,x)|\leq\bar{K}
for all
t\in[0,T]
. Finally, it is clear that
\theta
satisfies the other requirements of Theorem
2.9
and the existence of
\theta
is proved.
∎

Then next lemma shows the
L^{2}
-estimation of FBSDEP (
2.96
).

Lemma 2.7
.

Let
(A4)
hold, and
\theta(t,x)
satisfies the conditions in Theorem
2.9
. Then we obtain the
L^{2}
-estimations of FBSDEP (
2.96
)

\displaystyle E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}|x^{0,\xi}_{s}-x^{0,\tilde{\xi}}_{s}|^{2}+\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}|y^{0,\xi}_{s}-y^{0,\tilde{\xi}}_{s}|^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{0}^{T}|z^{i,0,\xi}_{s}-z^{i,0,\tilde{\xi}}_{s}|^{2}ds

(2.125)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|\tilde{z}^{i,0,\xi}_{(s,e)}-\tilde{z}^{i,0,\tilde{\xi}}_{(s,e)}|^{2}\nu_{i}(de)ds\bigg|\mathcal{F}_{0}\bigg]\leq C|\xi-\tilde{\xi}|^{2},

\displaystyle E\bigg[\sup_{t\leq s\leq T}|x^{0,\xi}_{s}-x^{0,\tilde{\xi}}_{s}|^{2}+\sup_{t\leq s\leq T}|y^{0,\xi}_{s}-y^{0,\tilde{\xi}}_{s}|^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{t}^{T}|z^{i,0,\xi}_{s}-z^{i,0,\tilde{\xi}}_{s}|^{2}ds

(2.126)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{t}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|\tilde{z}^{i,0,\xi}_{(s,e)}-\tilde{z}^{i,0,\tilde{\xi}}_{(s,e)}|^{2}\nu_{i}(de)ds\bigg|\mathcal{F}_{t}\bigg]\leq C|x^{0,\xi}_{t}-x^{0,\tilde{\xi}}_{t}|^{2},

where constant
C
depends on
\beta,K,\rho
and
L
in
(A4)
.

Proof.

Based on the idea in the proof of Theorem
2.4
, it is easy to prove the two estimations.
∎

Therefore, for any
u\in\mathcal{U}_{ad}[0,T]
and any given terminal time
T
, under
(A1)
and the conditions in Theorem
2.9
, and combining Theorem
2.9
, Lemma
2.7
and Theorem
2.4
, there exists a unique
L^{\beta}(\beta>2)
-solution
(x,y,z^{1},z^{2},\tilde{z}^{1},\tilde{z}^{2})
to state equation (
2.9
) on
[0,T]
.

3
Global Maximum Principle for Partially Observed Optimal Control Problem of FBSDEPs

In this section, we study the partially observed global maximum principe for the optimal control problem of FBSDEPs. The main result is an extension of the work in
[
8
]
and
[
38
]
.

3.1
Spike variation

We consider the state equation (
2.1
), observation equation

\left\{\begin{aligned} dY_{t}^{u}&=b_{2}(t,x_{t}^{u},u_{t})dt+\sigma_{3}(t)d\tilde{W}^{2}_{t}+\int_{\mathcal{E}_{2}}f_{3}(t,e)\tilde{N}^{\prime}_{2}(de,dt),\quad t\in[0,T],\\
Y_{0}^{u}&=0,\end{aligned}\right.

(3.1)

and the cost functional (
2.4
). Noting that
b_{2}
is independent of
(y,z^{1}_{t},z^{2}_{t},\tilde{z}^{1}_{(t,e)},\tilde{z}^{2}_{(t,e)})
here, comparing with (
2.3
). The general
b_{2}
case will not be considered in this section, which is our future research topic.

According to Section 2, we can transfer the partially observed optimal control problem into the complete information case with the controlled FBSDEP

\left\{\begin{aligned} dx_{t}^{u}&=\tilde{b}_{1}\big(t,x_{t}^{u},u_{t}\big)dt+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\sigma_{i}(t,x_{t}^{u},u_{t})dW^{i}_{t}+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}f_{i}(t,x_{t-}^{u},u_{t},e)\tilde{N}_{i}(de,dt),\\
-dy_{t}^{u}&=g\big(t,x_{t}^{u},y_{t}^{u},z^{1,u}_{t},z^{2,u}_{t},\tilde{z}^{1,u}_{(t,e)},\tilde{z}^{2,u}_{(t,e)},u_{t}\big)dt-\sum^{2}_{i=1}z^{i,u}_{t}dW^{i}_{t}-\sum^{2}_{i=1}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\tilde{z}^{i,u}_{(t,e)}\tilde{N}_{i}(de,dt),\quad t\in[0,T],\\
x_{0}^{u}&=x_{0},\quad y_{T}^{u}=\phi(x_{T}^{u}),\end{aligned}\right.

and (
2.7
), where
\tilde{b}_{1}
does not contain
(y,z^{1}_{t},z^{2}_{t},\tilde{z}^{1}_{(t,e)},\tilde{z}^{2}_{(t,e)})
, which admits a unique solution
(x^{u},y^{u},z^{1,u},z^{2,u},\tilde{z}^{1.u},\tilde{z}^{2,u},\tilde{\Gamma}^{u})
under
P
by
(A1)
.
Moreover, the cost functional (
2.4
) becomes

J(u)=E\bigg[\int_{0}^{T}\tilde{\Gamma}_{t}l\big(t,x_{t}^{u},y_{t}^{u},z^{1,u}_{t},z^{2,u}_{t},\tilde{z}^{1,u}_{(t,e)},\tilde{z}^{2,u}_{(t,e)},u_{t}\big)dt+\tilde{\Gamma}_{T}\Phi(x_{T}^{u})+\Gamma(y_{0}^{u})\bigg].

(3.2)

Since
U
is not necessarily convex, we apply the new spike variation technique
[
38
]
. Suppose that
\bar{u}\in\mathcal{U}_{ad}
is an optimal control, for any
\bar{t}\in[0,T]
, define
u^{\epsilon}
as follows. For
s\in[0,T]
,

\displaystyle u^{\epsilon}_{s}=\begin{cases}u,&\text{if $(s,\omega)\in\mathcal{O}:=\rrbracket\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon\rrbracket\backslash\bigcup_{n=1}^{\infty}\llbracket T_{n}\rrbracket$},\\
\bar{u}_{s},&\text{otherwise},\end{cases}

where
\llbracket T_{n}\rrbracket:=\big\{(\omega,t)\in\Omega\times[0,T]|T_{n}(\omega)=t\big\}
, which is the graph of stopping time
T_{n}
when the jump of the observable process appears, is a progressive set, and
u
is a bounded
\mathcal{F}^{Y}_{\bar{t}}
-measurable function taking values in
U
. The big difference is that the value of
u^{\epsilon}_{s}
at
T_{n}(\omega)
is equal to
\bar{u}_{s}
rather than
u
when some jump appears in
(\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]
, that is, some
T_{n}(\omega)
is in
(\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]
. Moreover, different from completely observed case and due to technical difficulties, we also assume that there is no jumps of the unobservable process appearing in
(\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]
. It is easy to show that
u^{\epsilon}\in\mathcal{U}_{ad}[0,T]
defined in (
2.5
).

Denoting by
(\bar{x},\bar{y},\bar{z}^{1},\bar{z}^{2},\bar{\tilde{z}}^{1},\bar{\tilde{z}}^{2})
the trajectory of
\bar{u}
and by
(x^{\epsilon},y^{\epsilon},z^{1,\epsilon},z^{2,\epsilon},\tilde{z}^{1,\epsilon},\tilde{z}^{2,\epsilon})
the trajectory of
u^{\epsilon}
. For simplicity, for
i=1,2
, for
f_{i},\varphi=\tilde{b}_{1},\sigma_{i},g,\phi
and
\kappa=x,y,z^{i},\tilde{z}^{i}
, denote

\displaystyle f_{i}(t,e)

\displaystyle=f_{i}(t,\bar{x}_{t},\bar{u}_{t},e),\quad f_{ix}(t,e)=f_{ix}(t,\bar{x}_{t},\bar{u}_{t},e),\quad\delta f_{i}(t,e)=f_{i}(t,\bar{x}_{t},u_{t},e)-f_{i}(t,e),

\displaystyle\delta f_{ix}(t,e)

\displaystyle=f_{ix}(t,\bar{x}_{t},u_{t},e)-f_{ix}(t,e),\quad\varphi(t)=\varphi(t,\bar{\Theta}(t),\bar{u}_{t}),\quad\varphi_{\kappa}(t)=\varphi_{\kappa}(t,\bar{\Theta}(t),\bar{u}_{t}),

\displaystyle\delta\varphi(t)

\displaystyle=\varphi(t,\bar{\Theta}(t),u_{t})-\varphi(t),\quad\delta\varphi_{\kappa}(t)=\varphi_{\kappa}(t,\bar{\Theta}(t),u_{t})-\varphi_{\kappa}(t),

\displaystyle\delta\varphi(t,\Delta^{1},\Delta^{2})

\displaystyle=\varphi(t,\bar{x}_{t},\bar{y}_{t},\bar{z}^{1}_{t}+\Delta^{1}(t),\bar{z}^{2}_{t}+\Delta^{2}(t),\bar{\tilde{z}}^{1},\bar{\tilde{z}}^{2},u_{t})-\varphi(t),

\displaystyle\delta\varphi_{\kappa}(t,\Delta^{1},\Delta^{2})

\displaystyle=\varphi_{\kappa}(t,\bar{x}_{t},\bar{y}_{t},\bar{z}^{1}_{t}+\Delta^{1}(t),\bar{z}^{2}_{t}+\Delta^{2}(t),\bar{\tilde{z}}^{1},\bar{\tilde{z}}^{2},u_{t})-\varphi_{\kappa}(t),

where
\Delta^{1}(\cdot),\Delta^{2}(\cdot)
are
\mathcal{F}_{t}
-adapted processes, to be determined later.

Denote
\bar{\Theta}(t):=(\bar{x}_{t},\bar{y}_{t},\bar{z}^{1}_{t},\bar{z}^{2}_{t},\bar{\tilde{z}}^{1}_{(t,e)},\bar{\tilde{z}}^{2}_{(t,e)})
and
\Theta^{\epsilon}(t):=(x^{\epsilon}_{t},y^{\epsilon}_{t},z^{1,\epsilon}_{t},z^{2,\epsilon}_{t},\tilde{z}^{1,\epsilon}_{(t,e)},\tilde{z}^{2,\epsilon}_{(t,e)})
, and set

\xi^{1,\epsilon}_{t}:=x^{\epsilon}_{t}-\bar{x}_{t},\ \ \eta^{1,\epsilon}_{t}:=y^{\epsilon}_{t}-\bar{y}_{t},\ \ \zeta^{i,1,\epsilon}_{t}:=z^{i,\epsilon}_{t}-\bar{z}^{i}_{t},\ \ \lambda^{i,1,\epsilon}_{(t,e)}:=\tilde{z}^{i,\epsilon}_{(t,e)}-\bar{\tilde{z}}^{i}_{(t,e)},\quad i=1,2,

(3.3)

we have

\left\{\begin{aligned} d\xi^{1,\epsilon}_{t}&=\big[\tilde{b}^{\epsilon}_{1x}(t)\xi^{1,\epsilon}_{t}+\delta\tilde{b}_{1}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}\big]dt+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\big[\tilde{\sigma}^{\epsilon}_{ix}(t)\xi^{1,\epsilon}_{t}+\delta\sigma_{i}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}\big]dW^{i}_{t}\\
&\quad+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\big[\tilde{f}^{\epsilon}_{ix}(t,e)\xi^{1,\epsilon}_{t-}+\delta f_{i}(t,e)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{O}}\big]\tilde{N}_{i}(de,dt),\\
d\eta^{1,\epsilon}_{t}&=-\bigg[\tilde{g}^{\epsilon}_{x}(t)\xi^{1,\epsilon}_{t}+\tilde{g}^{\epsilon}_{y}(t)\eta^{1,\epsilon}_{t}+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\bigg(\tilde{g}^{\epsilon}_{z^{i}}(t)\zeta^{i,1,\epsilon}_{t}+\tilde{g}^{\epsilon}_{\tilde{z}^{i}}(t)\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\lambda^{i,1,\epsilon}_{(t,e)}\nu_{i}(de)\bigg)\\
&\quad+\delta g(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}\bigg]dt+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\zeta^{i,1,\epsilon}_{t}dW^{i}_{t}+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\lambda^{i,1,\epsilon}_{(t,e)}\tilde{N}_{i}(de,dt),\quad t\in[0,T],\\
\xi^{1,\epsilon}_{0}&=0,\quad\eta^{1,\epsilon}_{T}=\tilde{\phi}_{x}^{\epsilon}(T)\xi^{1,\epsilon}_{T},\end{aligned}\right.

(3.4)

where

\displaystyle\tilde{b}^{\epsilon}_{1x}(t)

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{1}\tilde{b}_{1x}(t,\bar{x}_{t}+\theta(x^{\epsilon}_{t}-\bar{x}_{t}),u^{\epsilon}_{t})d\theta,

(3.5)

\displaystyle\tilde{g}^{\epsilon}_{x}(t)

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{1}g_{x}(t,\bar{\Theta}(t)+\theta(\Theta^{\epsilon}(t)-\bar{\Theta}(t)),u^{\epsilon}_{t})d\theta,

and
\tilde{\sigma}^{\epsilon}_{ix}(t),\tilde{f}^{\epsilon}_{ix}(t,e),\tilde{g}^{\epsilon}_{y}(t),\tilde{g}^{\epsilon}_{z^{i}}(t),\tilde{g}^{\epsilon}_{\tilde{z}^{i}}(t)
and
\tilde{\phi}_{x}^{\epsilon}(T),i=1,2
have similar definitions.

Then, by
L^{\beta}(\beta\geq 2)
-estimate of FBSDEPs in Section 2, we get

\displaystyle E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}(|\xi^{1,\epsilon}_{t}|^{\beta}+|\eta^{1,\epsilon}_{t}|^{\beta})+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|\zeta^{i,1,\epsilon}_{t}|^{2}dt\bigg)^{\frac{\beta}{2}}+\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|\lambda^{i,1,\epsilon}_{(t,e)}|^{2}\nu_{i}(de)dt\bigg)^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\bigg]\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq CE\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\delta g(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}dt\bigg)^{\beta}+\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\delta\tilde{b}_{1}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}dt\bigg)^{\beta}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|\delta\sigma_{i}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}|^{2}dt\bigg)^{\frac{\beta}{2}}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|\delta f_{i}(t,e)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{O}}|^{2}N_{i}(de,dt)\bigg)^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq CE\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}(1+|\bar{x}|+|\bar{y}|+|\bar{z}^{1}|+|\bar{z}^{2}|+||\bar{\tilde{z}}^{1}||+||\bar{\tilde{z}}^{2}||+|\bar{u}|+|u|)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}dt\bigg)^{\beta}

\displaystyle+\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}(1+|\bar{x}|+|\bar{u}|+|u|)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}dt\bigg)^{\beta}+\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}(1+|\bar{x}|+|\bar{u}|+|u|)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}dt\bigg)^{\frac{\beta}{2}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|\delta f_{i}(t,e)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{O}}|^{2}N_{i}(de,dt)\bigg)^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq CE\bigg[\bigg(\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}(1+|\bar{x}|+|\bar{y}|+|\bar{u}|+|u|)dt\bigg)^{\beta}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg(\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}|\bar{z}^{i}|dt\bigg)^{\beta}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg(\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}||\bar{\tilde{z}}^{i}||dt\bigg)^{\beta}+\bigg(\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}(1+|\bar{x}|+|\bar{u}|+|u|)dt\bigg)^{\beta}

\displaystyle+\bigg(\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}(1+|\bar{x}|+|\bar{u}|+|u|)^{2}dt\bigg)^{\frac{\beta}{2}}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|\delta f_{i}(t,e)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{O}}|^{2}N_{i}(de,dt)\bigg)^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq C\bigg\{\epsilon^{\beta-1}E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}(1+|\bar{x}|^{\beta}+|\bar{y}|^{\beta}+|\bar{u}|^{\beta}+|u|^{\beta})dt\bigg)\bigg]+\epsilon^{\frac{\beta}{2}}E\bigg[\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg(\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}|\bar{z}^{i}|^{2}dt\bigg)^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{\frac{\beta}{2}}E\bigg[\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg(\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|\bar{\tilde{z}}^{i}|^{2}\nu_{i}(de)dt\bigg)^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\bigg]+\epsilon^{\frac{\beta}{2}-1}E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}(1+|\bar{x}|^{\beta}+|\bar{u}|^{\beta}+|u|^{\beta})dt\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{2}E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|\delta f_{i}(t,e)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{O}}|^{2}N_{i}(de,dt)\bigg)^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\bigg]\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq C\bigg\{\epsilon^{\frac{\beta}{2}}+\epsilon^{\beta}+(\epsilon^{\beta}+\epsilon^{\frac{\beta}{2}})E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|\bar{x}|^{\beta}\bigg]+\epsilon^{\beta}E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|\bar{y}|^{\beta}\bigg]+(\epsilon^{\beta}+\epsilon^{\frac{\beta}{2}})\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}E|\bar{u}|^{\beta}

\displaystyle+(\epsilon^{\beta}+\epsilon^{\frac{\beta}{2}})\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}E|u|^{\beta}+\epsilon^{\frac{\beta}{2}}E\bigg[\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|\bar{z}^{i}|^{2}dt\bigg)^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\bigg]+\epsilon^{\frac{\beta}{2}}E\bigg[\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|\bar{\tilde{z}}^{i}|^{2}\nu_{i}(de)dt\bigg)^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{2}E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|\delta f_{i}(t,e)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{O}}|^{2}N_{i}(de,dt)\bigg)^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\bigg]\bigg\}\leq O(\epsilon^{\frac{\beta}{2}})+O(\epsilon^{\beta}),

where we have used that, under
(A1)
, by Theorem
2.2
,

E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|\bar{x}|^{\beta}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|\bar{y}|^{\beta}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|\bar{z}^{i}|^{2}dt\bigg)^{\frac{\beta}{2}}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|\bar{\tilde{z}}^{i}|^{2}\nu_{i}(de)dt\bigg)^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\bigg]<\infty.

Noticing that the estimate is derived by subtracting the jump part on
\mathcal{O}
, which means the jump term does not influence the order of variation. If not, the part with jump

E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|\delta f_{i}(t,e)|^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}N_{i}(de,dt)\bigg)^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\bigg],\ i=1,2,

is always of order
O(\epsilon)
, no matter how large
\beta
is. Indeed, we have

\displaystyle E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|\delta f_{i}(t,e)|^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}N_{i}(de,dt)\bigg)^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\bigg]\leq CE\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}(1+|\bar{x}|+|\bar{u}|+|u|)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}N_{i}({\mathcal{E}_{i}},dt)\bigg)^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\bigg].

Set
I_{s}:=(1+|\bar{x}_{s-}|+|\bar{u}|+|u|)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}
, and
J_{it}:=\int_{0}^{t}I_{s}N_{i}({\mathcal{E}_{i}},ds)
, then
J_{it}
is a pure jump process and so is
J_{it}^{\frac{\beta}{2}}
. Moreover, the jump time of
J_{i}^{\frac{\beta}{2}}
is also a jump time of
N_{i}
and the jump size of
N_{i}
is always equal to 1. Therefore we have

\displaystyle J_{iT}^{\frac{\beta}{2}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{s\leq T}\big(J_{is}^{\frac{\beta}{2}}-J_{is-}^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\big)=\sum_{s\leq T}\big(J_{is}^{\frac{\beta}{2}}-J_{is-}^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\big)\mathbbm{1}_{N_{i}(\mathcal{E}_{i},\{s\})\neq 0}=\sum_{s\leq T}\big(|J_{is-}+I_{s}|^{\frac{\beta}{2}}-J_{is-}^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\big)N_{i}(\mathcal{E}_{i},\{s\})

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{T}\big(|J_{is-}+I_{s}|^{\frac{\beta}{2}}-J_{is-}^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\big)N_{i}(\mathcal{E}_{i},ds)\leq C\int_{0}^{T}\big(J_{is-}^{\frac{\beta}{2}}+I_{s}^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\big)N_{i}(\mathcal{E}_{i},ds).

By the predictability of
J_{i\cdot-}
and
I
, we get

EJ_{iT}^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\leq CE\int_{0}^{T}\big(J_{is}^{\frac{\beta}{2}}+I_{s}^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\big)ds\leq CE\int_{0}^{T}J_{is}^{\frac{\beta}{2}}ds+CE\int_{0}^{T}(1+|\bar{x}_{s}|+|\bar{u}_{s}|+|u_{s}|)^{\beta}\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}ds.

From Gronwall’s inequality, we have

EJ_{iT}^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\leq CE\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}(1+|\bar{x}_{s}|+|\bar{u}_{s}|+|u_{s}|)^{\beta}ds\leq O(\epsilon).

Therefore, we derive

\displaystyle E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}(|\xi^{1,\epsilon}_{t}|^{\beta}+|\eta^{1,\epsilon}_{t}|^{\beta})+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|\zeta^{i,1,\epsilon}_{t}|^{2}dt\bigg)^{\frac{\beta}{2}}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|\lambda^{i,1,\epsilon}_{(t,e)}|^{2}\nu_{i}(de)dt\bigg)^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\bigg]

(3.6)

\displaystyle=O(\epsilon^{\frac{\beta}{2}})+O(\epsilon^{\beta}).

According to the estimate (
3.6
) in the above, we could set

\displaystyle x^{\epsilon}_{t}-\bar{x}_{t}=x^{1}_{t}+x^{2}_{t}+o(\epsilon),\quad y^{\epsilon}_{t}-\bar{y}_{t}=y^{1}_{t}+y^{2}_{t}+o(\epsilon),

(3.7)

\displaystyle z^{i,\epsilon}_{t}-\bar{z}^{i}_{t}=z^{i,1}_{t}+z^{i,2}_{t}+o(\epsilon),\quad\tilde{z}^{i,\epsilon}_{(t,e)}-\bar{\tilde{z}}^{i}_{(t,e)}=\tilde{z}^{i,1}_{(t,e)}+\tilde{z}^{i,2}_{(t,e)}+o(\epsilon),\ i=1,2,

where
x^{1},y^{1},z^{1,1},z^{2,1},\tilde{z}^{1,1},\tilde{z}^{2,1}\sim O(\sqrt{\epsilon})
and
x^{2},y^{2},z^{1,2},z^{2,2},\tilde{z}^{1,2},\tilde{z}^{2,2}\sim O(\epsilon)
.

3.2
Variational equations

Then, we introduce the first and second order variational equations for the SDEP as follows:

\displaystyle x^{1}_{t}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\tilde{b}_{1x}(s)x_{s}^{1}ds+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\int_{0}^{t}\big[\sigma_{ix}(s)x_{s}^{1}+\delta\sigma_{i}(s)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}\big]dW^{i}_{s}

(3.8)

\displaystyle+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathcal{\mathcal{E}}_{i}}f_{ix}(s,e)x_{s-}^{1}\tilde{N}_{i}(de,ds),

\displaystyle x^{2}_{t}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\Big[\tilde{b}_{1x}(s)x_{s}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\tilde{b}_{1xx}(s)(x_{s}^{1})^{2}+\delta\tilde{b}_{1}(s)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}\Big]ds

(3.9)

\displaystyle+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\int_{0}^{t}\Big[\sigma_{ix}(s)x_{s}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\sigma_{ixx}(s)(x_{s}^{1})^{2}+\delta\sigma_{ix}(s)x_{s}^{1}\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}\Big]dW^{i}_{s}

\displaystyle+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathcal{\mathcal{E}}_{i}}\Big[f_{ix}(s,e)x_{s-}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}f_{ixx}(s,e)(x_{s-}^{1})^{2}\Big]\tilde{N}_{i}(de,ds),

with
x^{1}_{0}=x^{2}_{0}=0
. Inspired by
[
8
]
(or
[
9
]
), we guess that
z_{t}^{i,1},\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,1},i=1,2
have the following forms, respectively:

z_{t}^{i,1}=\Delta^{i}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}+z_{t}^{i,1^{\prime}},\quad\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,1}=\tilde{\Delta}^{i}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{O}}+\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,1^{\prime}},\quad i=1,2,

(3.10)

where
\Delta^{1}(t),\Delta^{2}(t),\tilde{\Delta}^{1}(t),\tilde{\Delta}^{2}(t)
are
\mathcal{F}_{t}
-adapted processes, to be determined later, and importantly,
z_{t}^{i,1^{\prime}},\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,1^{\prime}},i=1,2
have good estimates similarly as
x_{t}^{1}
. Then

\displaystyle g\big(t,x^{\epsilon}_{t},y^{\epsilon}_{t},z^{1,\epsilon}_{t},z^{2,\epsilon}_{t},\tilde{z}^{1,\epsilon}_{(t,e)},\tilde{z}^{2,\epsilon}_{(t,e)},u^{\epsilon}_{t}\big)-g\big(t,\bar{x}_{t},\bar{y}_{t},\bar{z}^{1}_{t},\bar{z}^{2}_{t},\bar{\tilde{z}}^{1}_{(t,e)},\bar{\tilde{z}}^{2}_{(t,e)},\bar{u}_{t}\big)

\displaystyle=g\big(t,\bar{x}_{t}+x_{t}^{1}+x_{t}^{2},\bar{y}_{t}+y_{t}^{1}+y_{t}^{2},\bar{z}^{1}_{t}+\Delta^{1}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}+z_{t}^{1,1^{\prime}}+z_{t}^{1,2},

\displaystyle\bar{z}^{2}_{t}+\Delta^{2}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}+z_{t}^{2,1^{\prime}}+z_{t}^{2,2},\bar{\tilde{z}}^{1}_{(t,e)}+\tilde{\Delta}^{1}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{O}}+\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{1,1^{\prime}}+\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{1,2},

\displaystyle\bar{\tilde{z}}^{2}_{(t,e)}+\tilde{\Delta}^{2}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{O}}+\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{2,1^{\prime}}+\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{2,2},u^{\epsilon}_{t}\big)-g(t)+o(\epsilon)

\displaystyle=g_{x}(t)(x_{t}^{1}+x_{t}^{2})+g_{y}(t)(y_{t}^{1}+y_{t}^{2})+\sum_{i=1}^{2}g_{z^{i}}(t)(z_{t}^{i,1^{\prime}}+z_{t}^{i,2})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{2}g_{\tilde{z}^{i}}(t)\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}(\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,1^{\prime}}+\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,2})\nu_{i}(de)+\frac{1}{2}\Xi(t)D^{2}g(t)\Xi(t)^{\top}

\displaystyle+\delta g(t,\Delta^{1},\Delta^{2},\tilde{\Delta}^{1},\tilde{\Delta}^{2})\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}+o(\epsilon),

where
\Xi(t):=\big[x_{t}^{1},y_{t}^{1},z_{t}^{1,1^{\prime}},z_{t}^{2,1^{\prime}},\int_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{1,1^{\prime}}\nu_{1}(de),\int_{\mathcal{E}_{2}}\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{2,1^{\prime}}\nu_{2}(de)\big]
for simplicity.
So we can obtain the following equation for the BSDEP:

\left\{\begin{aligned} -d(y_{t}^{1}+y_{t}^{2})&=\bigg\{g_{x}(t)(x_{t}^{1}+x_{t}^{2})+g_{y}(t)(y_{t}^{1}+y_{t}^{2})+\sum^{2}_{i=1}g_{z^{i}}(t)(z_{t}^{i,1^{\prime}}+z_{t}^{i,2})\\
&\qquad+\sum^{2}_{i=1}g_{\tilde{z}^{i}}(t)\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}(\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,1^{\prime}}+\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,2})\nu_{i}(de)+\delta g(t,\Delta^{1},\Delta^{2},\tilde{\Delta}^{1},\tilde{\Delta}^{2})\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}\\
&\qquad+\frac{1}{2}\Xi(t)D^{2}g\Xi(t)^{\top}\bigg\}dt\\
&\quad-\sum^{2}_{i=1}(z_{t}^{i,1}+z_{t}^{i,2})dW^{i}_{t}-\sum^{2}_{i=1}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}(\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,1}+\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,2})\tilde{N}_{i}(de,dt),\quad t\in[0,T],\\
y_{T}^{1}+y_{T}^{2}&=\phi_{x}(\bar{x}_{T})(x_{T}^{1}+x_{T}^{2})+\frac{1}{2}\phi_{xx}(\bar{x}_{T})(x_{T}^{1})^{2},\end{aligned}\right.

(3.11)

Next, we need derive the corresponding first and second order variational equations for the BSDEP. Noticing that the terminal value in (
3.11
),
y_{T}^{1}=\phi_{x}(\bar{x}_{T})x_{T}^{1}
, we guess that
y_{t}^{1}=p_{t}x_{t}^{1}
, where
(p_{t},q^{1}_{t},q^{2}_{t},\tilde{q}^{1}_{(t,e)},\tilde{q}^{2}_{(t,e)})
satisfies the following adjoint equation:

\left\{\begin{aligned} -dp_{t}&=F(t)dt-\sum_{i=1}^{2}q^{i}_{t}dW^{i}_{t}-\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\tilde{q}^{i}_{(t,e)}\tilde{N}_{i}(de,dt),\quad t\in[0,T],\\
p_{T}&=\phi_{x}(\bar{x}_{T}),\end{aligned}\right.

where the
\mathcal{F}_{t}
-adapted process
F(\cdot)
is to be determined.

Applying Itô’s formula to
p_{t}x_{t}^{1}
, we can get

\left\{\begin{aligned} -dy_{t}^{1}&=\bigg\{g_{x}(t)x_{t}^{1}+g_{y}(t)y_{t}^{1}+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\bigg[g_{z^{i}}(t)(z_{t}^{i,1}-\Delta^{i}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]})\\
&\qquad+g_{\tilde{z}^{i}}(t)\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\big(\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,1}-\tilde{\Delta}^{i}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{O}}\big)\nu_{i}(de)\bigg]-\sum^{2}_{i=1}q^{i}_{t}\delta\sigma_{i}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}\bigg\}dt\\
&\quad-\sum^{2}_{i=1}z_{t}^{i,1}dW^{i}_{t}-\sum^{2}_{i=1}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,1}\tilde{N}_{i}(de,dt),\quad t\in[0,T],\\
y_{T}^{1}&=\phi_{x}(\bar{x}_{T})x_{T}^{1}.\end{aligned}\right.

(3.12)

and
(p_{t},q^{1}_{t},q^{2}_{t},\tilde{q}^{1}_{(t,e)},\tilde{q}^{2}_{(t,e)})\in\mathcal{N}^{\beta}[0,T]
is the unique solution to the following first order adjoint equation:

\left\{\begin{aligned} -dp_{t}&=\bigg\{\tilde{b}_{1x}(t)p_{t}+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\sigma_{ix}(t)q^{i}_{t}+g_{x}(t)+g_{y}(t)p_{t}+\sum^{2}_{i=1}g_{z^{i}}(t)k^{i}_{1}(t)\\
&\qquad+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\bigg[g_{\tilde{z}^{i}}(t)\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}k^{i}_{2}(t,e)\nu_{i}(de)+\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\mathbb{E}_{i}\big[f_{ix}(t,e)\big|\mathcal{P}\otimes\mathcal{B}(\mathcal{E}_{i})\big]\tilde{q}^{i}_{(t,e)}\nu_{i}(de)\bigg]\bigg\}dt\\
&\quad-\sum^{2}_{i=1}q^{i}_{t}dW^{i}_{t}-\sum^{2}_{i=1}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\tilde{q}^{i}_{(t,e)}\tilde{N}_{i}(de,dt),\quad t\in[0,T],\\
p_{T}&=\phi_{x}(\bar{x}_{T}).\end{aligned}\right.

(3.13)

From the deduction above, it is not hard to get the following relationships:

y_{t}^{1}=p_{t}x_{t}^{1},\quad z_{t}^{i,1}=k^{i}_{1}(t)x_{t}^{1}+p_{t}\delta\sigma_{i}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]},\quad\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,1}=k^{i}_{2}(t,e)x_{t-}^{1},\ i=1,2,

(3.14)

where

\displaystyle k^{i}_{1}(t)

\displaystyle:=\sigma_{ix}(t)p_{t}+q^{i}_{t},\quad k^{i}_{2}(t,e):=f_{ix}(t,e)p_{t-}+\tilde{q}^{i}_{(t,e)}+f_{ix}(t,e)\tilde{q}^{i}_{(t,e)},\ i=1,2.

(3.15)

Observing (
3.10
), we obtain

\displaystyle z_{t}^{i,1^{\prime}}=k^{i}_{1}(t)x_{t}^{1},\quad\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,1^{\prime}}=k^{i}_{2}(t,e)x_{t-}^{1},\quad\Delta^{i}(t)=p_{t}\delta\sigma_{i}(t),\quad\tilde{\Delta}^{i}(t)=0,\ i=1,2.

(3.16)

Remark 3.1
.

We notice that, different from the continuous martingale “
dW^{i}
” terms
z_{t}^{i,1}
,
\tilde{\Delta}^{i}(t)=0,i=1,2
, that is
\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,1}=\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,1^{\prime}},i=1,2,
which means the “jump term” in the first order variation has no change. Indeed, inspired by
[
8
]
(or
[
9
]
), the diffusion term of the first order variational equation of
x
should include the term
\delta\sigma_{i}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}
, so it is natural to guess another form of
z_{t}^{i,1}
in (
3.10
). However, considering the first order variational equation of
x
in our paper, motivated by the new variation technique in
[
38
]
, the “jump term” does not include the similar term
``\delta f_{i}(t,e)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{O}}"
. Therefore, the form of
\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,1}
has no need to change like
z_{t}^{i,1}
in heuristic derivation, which has been checked in the above discussion.

From (
3.11
) and (
3.12
), we introduce the first and second variational equations of the BSDEP as follows:

\left\{\begin{aligned} -dy_{t}^{1}&=\Big\{g_{x}(t)x_{t}^{1}+g_{y}(t)y_{t}^{1}+\sum^{2}_{i=1}g_{z^{i}}(t)\big(z_{t}^{i,1}-p_{t}\delta\sigma_{i}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}\big)+\sum^{2}_{i=1}g_{\tilde{z}^{i}}(t)\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,1}\nu_{i}(de)\\
&\quad-\sum^{2}_{i=1}q^{i}_{t}\delta\sigma_{i}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}\Big\}dt-\sum^{2}_{i=1}z_{t}^{i,1}dW^{i}_{t}-\sum^{2}_{i=1}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,1}\tilde{N}_{i}(de,dt),\\
-dy_{t}^{2}&=\bigg\{g_{x}(t)x_{t}^{2}+g_{y}(t)y_{t}^{2}+\sum^{2}_{i=1}g_{z^{i}}(t)z_{t}^{i,2}+\sum^{2}_{i=1}g_{\tilde{z}^{i}}(t)\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,2}\nu_{i}(de)+\frac{1}{2}\tilde{\Xi}(t)D^{2}g\tilde{\Xi}(t)^{\top}\\
&\quad+\delta g(t,p_{t}\delta\sigma_{1}(t),p_{t}\delta\sigma_{2}(t))\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}+\sum^{2}_{i=1}q^{i}_{t}\delta\sigma_{i}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}\bigg\}dt\\
&\quad-\sum^{2}_{i=1}z_{t}^{i,2}dW^{i}_{t}-\sum^{2}_{i=1}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,2}\tilde{N}_{i}(de,dt),\quad t\in[0,T],\\
y_{T}^{1}&=\phi_{x}(\bar{x}_{T})x_{T}^{1},\ y_{T}^{2}=\phi_{x}(\bar{x}_{T})x_{T}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\phi_{xx}(\bar{x}_{T})(x_{T}^{1})^{2},\end{aligned}\right.

(3.17)

where
\tilde{\Xi}(t)=\big[x_{t}^{1},y_{t}^{1},z_{t}^{1,1}-p_{t}\delta\sigma_{1}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]},z_{t}^{2,1}-p_{t}\delta\sigma_{2}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]},\int_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{1,1}\nu_{1}(de),\int_{\mathcal{E}_{2}}\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{2,1}\nu_{2}(de)\big]
. It is easy to check that the first and second order variational equations (
3.8
), (
3.17
) admit unique solutions
x^{1},x^{2}
,
(y^{1},z^{1,1},z^{2,1},\tilde{z}^{1,1},\tilde{z}^{2,1})
and
(y^{2},z^{1,2},z^{2,2},\tilde{z}^{1,2},\tilde{z}^{2,2})
, respectively.

Lemma 3.1
.

Let
(A1)
hold, then we have

\displaystyle y_{t}^{1}

\displaystyle=p_{t}x_{t}^{1},\quad z_{t}^{i,1}=k^{i}_{1}(t)x_{t}^{1}+p_{t}\delta\sigma_{i}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]},\quad\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,1}=k^{i}_{2}(t,e)x_{t-}^{1},\ i=1,2,

(3.18)

where
p_{t}
satisfies (
3.13
), and
k^{i}_{1}(t),k^{i}_{2}(t,e),i=1,2,
are given by (
3.15
).

Proof.

We introduce the following SDEP:

\left\{\begin{aligned} d\hat{x}_{t}^{1}&=\tilde{b}_{1x}(t)\hat{x}_{t}^{1}dt+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\big[\sigma_{ix}(t)\hat{x}_{t}^{1}+\delta\sigma_{i}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}\big]dW^{i}_{t}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}f_{ix}(t,e)\hat{x}_{t-}^{1}\tilde{N}_{i}(de,dt),\quad t\in[0,T],\\
\hat{x}_{0}^{1}&=0,\end{aligned}\right.

which admits a unique solution
\hat{x}_{t}^{1}
, and set

\hat{y}_{t}^{1}:=p_{t}\hat{x}_{t}^{1},\quad\hat{z}_{t}^{i,1}:=k^{i}_{1}(t)\hat{x}_{t}^{1}+p_{t}\delta\sigma_{i}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]},\quad\hat{\tilde{z}}_{(t,e)}^{i,1}:=k^{i}_{2}(t,e)\hat{x}_{t-}^{1},\ i=1,2.

Applying Itô’s formula to
p_{t}\hat{x}_{t}^{1}
, we can get

\displaystyle\hat{y}_{t}^{1}

\displaystyle=\phi_{\hat{x}}(\hat{\bar{x}}_{T})\hat{x}_{T}^{1}+\int_{t}^{T}\Big\{g_{\hat{x}}(s)\hat{x}_{s}^{1}+g_{\hat{y}}(s)\hat{y}_{s}^{1}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}g_{\hat{z}^{i}}(s)k^{i}_{1}(s)\hat{x}_{s}^{1}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}g_{\hat{\tilde{z}}^{i}}(s)\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}k^{i}_{2}(s,e)\nu_{i}(de)\hat{x}_{s}^{1}

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{2}q^{i}_{s}\delta\sigma_{i}(s)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}\Big\}ds-\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{t}^{T}\hat{z}_{s}^{i,1}dW^{i}_{s}-\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{t}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\hat{\tilde{z}}_{(s,e)}^{i,1}\tilde{N}_{i}(de,ds).

It is easy to check that
(\hat{x}^{1},\hat{y}^{1},\hat{z}^{1,1},\hat{z}^{2,1},\hat{\tilde{z}}^{1,1},\hat{\tilde{z}}^{2,1})
solves (
3.8
) and (
3.17
), and by Theorem
2.6
,
(\hat{x}^{1},\hat{y}^{1},\hat{z}^{1,1},\hat{z}^{2,1},\hat{\tilde{z}}^{1,1},\hat{\tilde{z}}^{2,1})=(x^{1},y^{1},z^{1,1},z^{2,1},\tilde{z}^{1,1},\tilde{z}^{2,1})
. The proof is complete.
∎

3.3
First-order and second-order expansions

The following standard estimates of FBSDEPs are needed.

Lemma 3.2
.

Suppose that
(A1)
holds, for
\beta\geq 2
, we have the following estimates:

\displaystyle E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}(|x_{t}^{1}|^{\beta}+|y_{t}^{1}|^{\beta})+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|z_{t}^{i,1}|^{2}dt\bigg)^{\frac{\beta}{2}}+\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,1}|^{2}\nu_{i}(de)dt\bigg)^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\bigg]\bigg]=O(\epsilon^{\frac{\beta}{2}}),

(3.19)

\displaystyle E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|x_{t}^{\epsilon}-\bar{x}_{t}-x_{t}^{1}|^{2}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|y_{t}^{\epsilon}-\bar{y}_{t}-y_{t}^{1}|^{2}+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\int_{0}^{T}|z_{t}^{i,\epsilon}-\bar{z}^{i}_{t}-z_{t}^{i,1}|^{2}dt\bigg]

\displaystyle+E\bigg[\sum^{2}_{i=1}\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,\epsilon}-\bar{\tilde{z}}^{i}_{(t,e)}-\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,1}|^{2}\nu_{i}(de)dt\bigg]=o(\epsilon),

\displaystyle E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|x_{t}^{\epsilon}-\bar{x}_{t}-x_{t}^{1}|^{4}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|y_{t}^{\epsilon}-\bar{y}_{t}-y_{t}^{1}|^{4}+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|z_{t}^{i,\epsilon}-\bar{z}^{i}_{t}-z_{t}^{i,1}|^{2}dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle+E\bigg[\sum^{2}_{i=1}\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,\epsilon}-\bar{\tilde{z}}^{i}_{(t,e)}-\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,1}|^{2}\nu_{i}(de)dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]=o(\epsilon^{2}).

Proof.

By
(A1)
, for any
\beta\geq 2
, we have

E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|p_{t}|^{\beta}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|q^{i}_{t}|^{2}dt\bigg)^{\frac{\beta}{2}}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|\tilde{q}^{i}_{(t,e)}|^{2}\nu_{i}(de)dt\bigg)^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\bigg]<\infty.

and thus

\displaystyle E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|x_{t}^{1}|^{\beta}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|y_{t}^{1}|^{\beta}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|z_{t}^{i,1}|^{2}dt\bigg)^{\frac{\beta}{2}}+\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,1}|^{2}\nu_{i}(de)dt\bigg)^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\bigg]\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq C_{\beta}E\bigg\{\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|g_{z^{i}}(t)p_{t}\delta\sigma_{i}(t)|\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}+|q^{i}_{t}\delta\sigma_{i}(t)|\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}dt\bigg)^{\beta}

\displaystyle+\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|\delta\sigma_{i}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}|^{2}dt\bigg)^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\bigg]\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\beta}\bigg\{E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}(1+|\bar{x}|+|\bar{u}|+|u|)^{2}dt\bigg)^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\bigg]+E\bigg[\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|g_{z^{i}}(t)p_{t}\delta\sigma_{i}(t)|\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}dt\bigg)^{\beta}\bigg]

\displaystyle+E\bigg[\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|q^{i}_{t}\delta\sigma_{i}(t)|\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}dt\bigg)^{\beta}\bigg]\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\beta}\bigg\{E\bigg[\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|p_{t}\delta\sigma_{i}(t)|\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}dt\bigg)^{\beta}\bigg]+E\bigg[\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|q^{i}_{t}\delta\sigma_{i}(t)|\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}dt\bigg)^{\beta}\bigg]\bigg\}+O(\epsilon^{\frac{\beta}{2}})

\displaystyle\leq C_{\beta}\bigg\{E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|p_{t}|^{\beta}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg(\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}|\delta\sigma_{i}(t)|dt\bigg)^{\beta}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{2}E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}|q^{i}_{t}|^{2}dt\bigg)^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\bigg(\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}|\delta\sigma_{i}(t)|^{2}dt\bigg)^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\bigg]\bigg\}+O(\epsilon^{\frac{\beta}{2}})

\displaystyle\leq C_{\beta}\bigg\{\bigg\{E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|p_{t}|^{2\beta}\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bigg\{E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}(1+|\bar{x}|+|\bar{u}|+|u|)dt\bigg)^{2\beta}\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg\{E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}|q^{i}_{t}|^{2}dt\bigg)^{\beta}\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bigg\{E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}(1+|\bar{x}|+|\bar{u}|+|u|)^{2}dt\bigg)^{\beta}\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bigg\}+O(\epsilon^{\frac{\beta}{2}})

\displaystyle\leq C_{\beta}\bigg\{\epsilon^{\beta-\frac{1}{2}}\bigg\{E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}(1+|\bar{x}|^{2\beta}+|\bar{u}|^{2\beta}+|u|^{2\beta})dt\bigg)\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\epsilon^{\frac{\beta}{2}-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg\{E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}|q^{i}_{t}|^{2}dt\bigg)^{\beta}\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\times\bigg\{E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}(1+|\bar{x}|^{2\beta}+|\bar{u}|^{2\beta}+|u|^{2\beta})dt\bigg)\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bigg\}+O(\epsilon^{\frac{\beta}{2}})

\displaystyle\leq C_{\beta}\bigg\{\epsilon^{\beta}+\epsilon^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg\{E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}|q^{i}_{t}|^{2}dt\bigg)^{\beta}\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bigg\}+O(\epsilon^{\frac{\beta}{2}})\leq O(\epsilon^{\frac{\beta}{2}}).

The first estimate in (
3.19
) holds. We continue to use the notations
\xi_{t}^{1,\epsilon},\eta_{t}^{1,\epsilon},\zeta_{t}^{1,1,\epsilon},\zeta_{t}^{2,1,\epsilon},\lambda_{(t,e)}^{1,1,\epsilon}
and
\lambda_{(t,e)}^{2,1,\epsilon}
in (
3.3
) and let

\displaystyle\xi_{t}^{2,\epsilon}:=x^{\epsilon}_{t}-\bar{x}_{t}-x_{t}^{1},\quad\eta_{t}^{2,\epsilon}:=y^{\epsilon}_{t}-\bar{y}_{t}-y_{t}^{1},\quad\zeta_{t}^{i,2,\epsilon}:=z^{i,\epsilon}_{t}-\bar{z}^{i}_{t}-z_{t}^{i,1},

(3.20)

\displaystyle\lambda_{(t,e)}^{i,2,\epsilon}:=\tilde{z}^{i,\epsilon}_{(t,e)}-\bar{\tilde{z}}^{i}_{(t,e)}-\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,1},\ i=1,2,\quad\Theta(t,\Delta^{1}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]},\Delta^{2}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]})

\displaystyle:=(\bar{x}_{t},\bar{y}_{t},\bar{z}^{1}_{t}+\Delta^{1}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]},\bar{z}^{2}_{t}+\Delta^{2}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]},\bar{\tilde{z}}^{1}_{(t,e)},\bar{\tilde{z}}^{2}_{(t,e)}),

thus

\left\{\begin{aligned} d\xi_{t}^{2,\epsilon}&=\Big[\tilde{b}_{1x}^{\epsilon}(t)\xi_{t}^{2,\epsilon}+(\tilde{b}_{1x}^{\epsilon}(t)-\tilde{b}_{1x}(t))x^{1}_{t}+\delta\tilde{b}_{1}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}\Big]dt\\
&\quad+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\Big[\tilde{\sigma}_{ix}^{\epsilon}(t)\xi_{t}^{2,\epsilon}+(\tilde{\sigma}_{ix}^{\epsilon}(t)-\sigma_{ix}(t))x^{1}_{t}\Big]dW^{i}_{t}\\
&\quad+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\Big[\tilde{f}_{ix}^{\epsilon}(t,e)\xi_{t-}^{2,\epsilon}+(\tilde{f}_{ix}^{\epsilon}(t,e)-f_{ix}(t,e))x^{1}_{t-}+\delta f_{i}(t,e)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{O}}\Big]\tilde{N}_{i}(de,dt),\\
d\eta_{t}^{2,\epsilon}&=-\Big[\tilde{g}_{x}^{\epsilon}(t)\xi_{t}^{2,\epsilon}+\tilde{g}_{y}^{\epsilon}(t)\eta_{t}^{2,\epsilon}+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\tilde{g}_{z^{i}}^{\epsilon}(t)\zeta_{t}^{i,2,\epsilon}+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\tilde{g}_{\tilde{z}^{i}}^{\epsilon}(t)\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\lambda_{(t,e)}^{i,2,\epsilon}\nu_{i}(de)+A_{1}^{\epsilon}(t)\Big]dt\\
&\quad+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\zeta_{t}^{i,2,\epsilon}dW^{i}_{t}+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\lambda_{(t,e)}^{i,2,\epsilon}\tilde{N}_{i}(de,dt),\quad t\in[0,T],\\
\xi_{0}^{2,\epsilon}&=0,\quad\eta_{T}^{2,\epsilon}=\tilde{\phi}_{x}^{\epsilon}(T)\xi_{T}^{2,\epsilon}+(\tilde{\phi}_{x}^{\epsilon}(T)-\phi_{x}(\bar{x}_{T}))x_{T}^{1},\end{aligned}\right.

where
\tilde{b}_{1x}^{\epsilon}(t),\tilde{\sigma}_{ix}^{\epsilon}(t),\tilde{f}_{ix}^{\epsilon}(t,e),\tilde{g}_{x}^{\epsilon}(t),\tilde{g}_{y}^{\epsilon}(t),\tilde{g}_{z^{i}}^{\epsilon}(t),\tilde{g}_{\tilde{z}^{i}}^{\epsilon}(t)
and
\tilde{\phi}_{x}^{\epsilon}(T)
are defined in (
3.5
), and

\displaystyle A_{1}^{\epsilon}(t)

\displaystyle:=\delta g(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}+\big[\tilde{g}_{x}^{\epsilon}(t)-g_{x}(t)\big]x_{t}^{1}+\big[\tilde{g}_{y}^{\epsilon}(t)-g_{y}(t)\big]y_{t}^{1}+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\Big[\big[\tilde{g}_{z^{i}}^{\epsilon}(t)-g_{z^{i}}(t)\big]z_{t}^{i,1}

\displaystyle+\big[\tilde{g}_{\tilde{z}^{i}}^{\epsilon}(t)-g_{\tilde{z}^{i}}(t)\big]\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,1}\nu_{i}(de)+g_{z^{i}}(t)\Delta^{i}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}+q^{i}_{t}\delta\sigma_{i}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}\Big].

By the
L^{\beta}
-estimate in Section 2, we have

\displaystyle E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}(|\xi^{2,\epsilon}_{t}|^{2}+|\eta^{2,\epsilon}_{t}|^{2})+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\bigg[\int_{0}^{T}|\zeta^{i,2,\epsilon}_{t}|^{2}dt+\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|\lambda^{i,2,\epsilon}_{(t,e)}|^{2}\nu_{i}(de)dt\bigg]\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq C_{\beta}E\bigg[\big|(\tilde{\phi}_{x}^{\epsilon}(T)-\phi_{x}(\bar{x}_{T}))x_{T}^{1}\big|^{2}+\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}A_{1}^{\epsilon}(t)dt\bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle+\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\big|(\tilde{b}_{1x}^{\epsilon}(t)-\tilde{b}_{1x}(t))x^{1}_{t}+\delta\tilde{b}_{1}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}\big|dt\bigg)^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\big|(\tilde{\sigma}_{ix}^{\epsilon}(t)-\sigma_{ix}(t))x^{1}_{t}\big|^{2}dt\bigg)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\big|(\tilde{f}_{ix}^{\epsilon}(t,e)-f_{ix}(t,e))x^{1}_{t-}+\delta f_{i}(t,e)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{O}}\big|^{2}N_{i}(de,dt)\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq C_{\beta}\bigg\{E\bigg[\bigg|\int_{0}^{1}\phi_{x}(\bar{x}_{T}+\theta(x^{\epsilon}_{T}-\bar{x}_{T}))-\phi_{x}(\bar{x}_{T})d\theta\bigg|^{2}|x_{T}^{1}|^{2}\bigg]+E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}A_{1}^{\epsilon}(t)dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle+E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|(\tilde{b}_{1x}^{\epsilon}(t)-\tilde{b}_{1x}(t))x^{1}_{t}+\delta\tilde{b}_{1}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}|dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{2}E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}(\tilde{\sigma}_{ix}^{\epsilon}(t)-\sigma_{ix}(t))^{2}|x^{1}_{t}|^{2}dt\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{2}E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}(\tilde{f}_{ix}^{\epsilon}(t,e)-f_{ix}(t,e))^{2}|x^{1}_{t-}|^{2}+(\delta f_{i}(t,e))^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{O}}N_{i}(de,dt)\bigg)\bigg]\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\beta,T}\bigg\{\bigg[E\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|\xi^{1,\epsilon}_{t}|^{4}\bigg]^{\frac{1}{2}}\bigg[E\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|x_{t}^{1}|^{4}\bigg]^{\frac{1}{2}}+E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}A_{1}^{\epsilon}(t)dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle+E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}(|\xi^{1,\epsilon}_{t}|^{2}+|\delta\tilde{b}_{1x}(t)|^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]})|x^{1}_{t}|^{2}dt+\epsilon\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}|\delta\tilde{b}_{1}(t)|^{2}dt\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{2}E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}(|\xi^{1,\epsilon}_{t}|^{2}+|\delta\sigma_{ix}(t)|^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]})|x^{1}_{t}|^{2}dt\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{2}E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}(|\xi^{1,\epsilon}_{t-}|^{2}+|\delta f_{ix}(t,e)|^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{O}})|x^{1}_{t-}|^{2}+(\delta f_{i}(t,e))^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{O}}N_{i}(de,dt)\bigg)\bigg]\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\beta,T}\bigg\{\epsilon^{2}+E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}A_{1}^{\epsilon}(t)dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]+\sum_{i=1}^{2}E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|x^{1}_{t}|^{2}\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}|\delta\sigma_{ix}(t)|^{2}dt\bigg]

\displaystyle+E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|\xi^{1,\epsilon}_{t}|^{2}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|x^{1}_{t}|^{2}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|x^{1}_{t}|^{2}\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}|\delta\tilde{b}_{1x}(t)|^{2}dt+\epsilon\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}|\delta\tilde{b}_{1}(t)|^{2}dt\bigg]

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{2}E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|x^{1}_{t-}|^{2}|\delta f_{ix}(t,e)|^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{O}}+(\delta f_{i}(t,e))^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{O}}N_{i}(de,dt)\bigg)\bigg]\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\beta,T}\bigg\{\epsilon^{2}+E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}A_{1}^{\epsilon}(t)dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]+\epsilon E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|x^{1}_{t}|^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg[E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|\xi^{1,\epsilon}_{t}|^{4}\bigg]\bigg]^{\frac{1}{2}}\bigg[E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|x^{1}_{t}|^{4}\bigg]\bigg]^{\frac{1}{2}}+\epsilon E\bigg[\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}(1+|\bar{x}|^{2}+|\bar{u}|^{2}+|u|^{2})dt\bigg]\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\beta,T}\bigg\{\epsilon^{2}+E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}A_{1}^{\epsilon}(t)dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]\bigg\}.

Next, we give the estimate of
E\big[\big(\int_{0}^{T}A_{1}^{\epsilon}(t)dt\big)^{2}\big]
. In fact, by
(A1)
, we first have

\displaystyle E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}x_{t}^{1}(\tilde{g}_{x}^{\epsilon}(t)-g_{x}(t))dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]\leq\bigg\{E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|x_{t}^{1}|^{4}\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bigg\{E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}(\tilde{g}_{x}^{\epsilon}(t)-g_{x}(t))dt\bigg)^{4}\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon\bigg\{E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\tilde{g}_{xx}^{\epsilon}(t)\xi_{t}^{1,\epsilon}+\tilde{g}_{xy}^{\epsilon}(t)\eta_{t}^{1,\epsilon}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg[\tilde{g}_{xz^{i}}^{\epsilon}(t)\zeta_{t}^{i,1,\epsilon}+\tilde{g}_{x\tilde{z}^{i}}^{\epsilon}(t)\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\lambda_{(t,e)}^{i,1,\epsilon}\nu_{i}(de)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\delta g_{x}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}dt\bigg)^{4}\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon C\bigg\{E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\xi_{t}^{1,\epsilon}+\eta_{t}^{1,\epsilon}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg[\zeta_{t}^{i,1,\epsilon}+\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\lambda_{(t,e)}^{i,1,\epsilon}\nu_{i}(de)\bigg]+\delta g_{x}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}dt\bigg)^{4}\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon C\bigg\{E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\big(|\xi_{t}^{1,\epsilon}|^{4}+|\eta_{t}^{1,\epsilon}|^{4}\big)+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|\zeta_{t}^{i,1,\epsilon}|^{2}dt\bigg)^{2}+\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}||\lambda_{(t,e)}^{i,1,\epsilon}||^{2}dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{2}\bigg(\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}|\delta g_{x}(t)|^{2}dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon C\bigg\{\epsilon^{2}+\epsilon^{4}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|\zeta_{t}^{i,1,\epsilon}|^{2}dt\bigg)^{2}+\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|\lambda_{(t,e)}^{i,1,\epsilon}|^{2}\nu_{i}(de)dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq O(\epsilon^{2}),

where

\displaystyle\tilde{g}_{xa}^{\epsilon}(t)=\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{1}\theta g_{xa}(t,\bar{\Theta}+\lambda\theta(\Theta^{\epsilon}-\bar{\Theta}),u^{\epsilon})d\lambda d\theta,\ a=x,y,z^{1},z^{2},\tilde{z}^{1},\tilde{z}^{2},

and
\tilde{g}_{ya}^{\epsilon}(t),\tilde{g}_{z^{1}a}^{\epsilon}(t),\tilde{g}_{z^{2}a}^{\epsilon}(t),\tilde{g}_{\tilde{z}^{1}a}^{\epsilon}(t),\tilde{g}_{\tilde{z}^{2}a}^{\epsilon}(t)
, for
a=x,y,z^{1},z^{2},\tilde{z}^{1},\tilde{z}^{2}
are defined similarly. Moreover, we have, for i=1,2,

\displaystyle E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}z_{t}^{i,1}(\tilde{g}_{z^{i}}^{\epsilon}(t)-g_{z^{i}}(t))dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]\leq E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|z_{t}^{i,1}|^{2}dt\bigg)\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|(\tilde{g}_{z^{i}}^{\epsilon}(t)-g_{z^{i}}(t))|^{2}dt\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\{E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|z_{t}^{i,1}|^{2}dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bigg\{E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|(\tilde{g}_{z^{i}}^{\epsilon}(t)-g_{z^{i}}(t))|^{2}dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon\bigg\{E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|(\tilde{g}_{z^{i}}^{\epsilon}(t)-g_{z^{i}}(t))|^{2}dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq O(\epsilon^{2}),

and similarly,

\displaystyle E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}y_{t}^{1}(\tilde{g}_{y}^{\epsilon}(t)-g_{y}(t))dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]\leq O(\epsilon^{2}),

\displaystyle E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,1}\nu_{i}(de)(\tilde{g}_{\tilde{z}^{i}}^{\epsilon}(t)-g_{\tilde{z}^{i}}(t))dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]\leq O(\epsilon^{2}),\ i=1,2.

And

\displaystyle E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}g_{z^{i}}(t)p_{t}\delta\sigma_{i}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]\leq E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|p_{t}|^{2}\bigg(\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}\delta\sigma_{i}(t)dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\{E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|p_{t}|^{4}\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bigg\{E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}(1+|\bar{x}|+|\bar{u}|+|u|)dt\bigg)^{4}\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}C\bigg\{E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}(1+|\bar{x}|^{4}+|\bar{u}|^{4}+|u|^{4})dt\bigg)\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq O(\epsilon^{2}),\ i=1,2,

and similarly,

E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\delta g(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]\leq O(\epsilon^{2}).

However, we only have, for i=1,2,

\displaystyle E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}q^{i}_{t}\delta\sigma_{i}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\bigg\{E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}|q^{i}_{t}|^{2}dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bigg\{E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}(1+|\bar{x}|^{4}+|\bar{u}|^{4}+|u|^{4})dt\bigg)\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq o(\epsilon).

Thus, we get the second estimate in (
3.19
). The third estimate in (
3.19
) can be proved similarly. The proof is complete.
∎

Lemma 3.3
.

Suppose that
(A1)
holds, then for
\beta\geq 2
, we have the following estimates:

\displaystyle E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}(|x_{t}^{2}|^{2}+|y_{t}^{2}|^{2})+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\bigg[\int_{0}^{T}|z_{t}^{i,2}|^{2}dt+\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,2}|^{2}\nu_{i}(de)dt\bigg]\bigg]=o(\epsilon),

(3.21)

\displaystyle E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}(|x_{t}^{2}|^{\beta}+|y_{t}^{2}|^{\beta})+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|z_{t}^{i,2}|^{2}dt\bigg)^{\frac{\beta}{2}}+\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,2}|^{2}\nu_{i}(de)dt\bigg)^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\bigg]\bigg]=o(\epsilon^{\frac{\beta}{2}}),

\displaystyle E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|x_{t}^{\epsilon}-\bar{x}_{t}-x_{t}^{1}-x_{t}^{2}|^{2}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|y_{t}^{\epsilon}-\bar{y}_{t}-y_{t}^{1}-y_{t}^{2}|^{2}+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\bigg[\int_{0}^{T}|z_{t}^{i,\epsilon}-\bar{z}^{i}_{t}-z_{t}^{i,1}-z_{t}^{i,2}|^{2}dt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,\epsilon}-\bar{\tilde{z}}^{i}_{(t,e)}-\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,1}-\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,2}|^{2}\nu_{i}(de)dt\bigg]\bigg]=o(\epsilon^{2}).

Proof.

We can use the same technique in Lemma
3.2
, to obtain the first estimate. However, we should note that we fail to make the order reach
O(\epsilon^{2})
due to the appearance of
E\big[\big(\int_{0}^{T}q^{i}_{t}\delta\sigma_{i}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}dt\big)^{2}\big]
term, and so does the second estimate. Then, we give the proof of the third estimate directly.
Let

\displaystyle\xi_{t}^{3,\epsilon}:=x^{\epsilon}_{t}-\bar{x}_{t}-x_{t}^{1}-x_{t}^{2},\quad\eta_{t}^{3,\epsilon}:=y^{\epsilon}_{t}-\bar{y}_{t}-y_{t}^{1}-y_{t}^{2},

(3.22)

\displaystyle\zeta_{t}^{i,3,\epsilon}:=z^{i,\epsilon}_{t}-\bar{z}^{i}_{t}-z_{t}^{i,1}-z_{t}^{i,2},\quad\lambda_{(t,e)}^{i,3,\epsilon}:=\tilde{z}^{i,\epsilon}_{(t,e)}-\bar{\tilde{z}}^{i}_{(t,e)}-\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,1}-\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,2},\ i=1,2,

and recall that
\xi_{t}^{1,\epsilon},\eta_{t}^{1,\epsilon},\zeta_{t}^{i,1,\epsilon},\lambda_{(t,e)}^{i,1,\epsilon},\xi_{t}^{2,\epsilon},\eta_{t}^{2,\epsilon},\zeta_{t}^{i,2,\epsilon}
and
\lambda_{(t,e)}^{i,2,\epsilon},i=1,2
are defined in (
3.20
). Further, we define

\displaystyle\tilde{\Upsilon}_{xx}^{\epsilon}(t):=2\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{1}\theta\Upsilon_{xx}(t,\bar{x}+\lambda\theta(x^{\epsilon}-\bar{x}),u^{\epsilon})d\lambda d\theta,\mbox{ for }\Upsilon=\tilde{b}_{1},\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2},f_{1},f_{2},

\displaystyle\widetilde{D^{2}g}^{\epsilon}(t):=2\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{1}\theta D^{2}g(t,\Theta(t,\Delta^{1}\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]},\Delta^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]})+\lambda\theta(\Theta^{\epsilon}-\Theta(t,\Delta^{1}\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]},

\displaystyle\Delta^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]})),u^{\epsilon})d\lambda d\theta,

and

\widetilde{\phi}_{xx}^{\epsilon}(T):=2\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{1}\theta\phi_{xx}(\bar{x}_{T}+\lambda\theta(x_{T}^{\epsilon}-\bar{x}_{T}))d\lambda d\theta
,
then we have the following equation:

\left\{\begin{aligned} d\xi_{t}^{3,\epsilon}&=\big[\tilde{b}_{1x}(t)\xi_{t}^{3,\epsilon}+B_{1}^{\epsilon}(t)\big]dt+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\Big[\sigma_{ix}(t)\xi_{t}^{3,\epsilon}+C_{i1}^{\epsilon}(t)\Big]dW^{i}_{t}\\
&\quad+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\Big[f_{ix}(t,e)\xi_{t-}^{3,\epsilon}+D_{i1}^{\epsilon}(t,e)\Big]\tilde{N}_{i}(de,dt),\\
d\eta_{t}^{3,\epsilon}&=-\Big\{g_{x}(t)\xi_{t}^{3,\epsilon}+g_{y}(t)\eta_{t}^{3,\epsilon}+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\Big[g_{z^{i}}(t)\zeta_{t}^{i,3,\epsilon}+g_{\tilde{z}^{i}}(t)\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\lambda_{(t,e)}^{i,3,\epsilon}\nu_{i}(de)\Big]+A_{2}^{\epsilon}(t)\Big\}dt\\
&\quad+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\zeta_{t}^{i,3,\epsilon}dW^{i}_{t}+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\lambda_{(t,e)}^{i,3,\epsilon}\tilde{N}_{i}(de,dt),\quad t\in[0,T],\\
\xi_{0}^{3,\epsilon}&=0,\quad\eta_{T}^{3,\epsilon}=\phi_{x}(\bar{x}_{T})\xi_{T}^{3,\epsilon}+\frac{1}{2}\widetilde{\phi}_{xx}^{\epsilon}(T)(\xi_{T}^{1,\epsilon})^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\phi_{xx}(\bar{x}_{T})(x_{T}^{1})^{2},\end{aligned}\right.

(3.23)

where

\displaystyle B_{1}^{\epsilon}(t)

\displaystyle:=\delta\tilde{b}_{1x}(t)\xi_{t}^{1,\epsilon}\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}+\frac{1}{2}\tilde{b}^{\epsilon}_{1xx}(t)|\xi_{t}^{1,\epsilon}|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\tilde{b}_{1xx}(t)(x^{1}_{t})^{2},

\displaystyle C_{i1}^{\epsilon}(t)

\displaystyle:=\delta\sigma_{ix}(t)\xi_{t}^{2,\epsilon}\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}+\frac{1}{2}\tilde{\sigma}^{\epsilon}_{ixx}(t)|\xi_{t}^{1,\epsilon}|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\sigma_{ixx}(t)(x^{1}_{t})^{2},

\displaystyle D_{i1}^{\epsilon}(t,e)

\displaystyle:=\delta f_{ix}(t,e)\xi_{t}^{1,\epsilon}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{O}}+\frac{1}{2}\tilde{f}^{\epsilon}_{ixx}(t,e)|\xi_{t}^{1,\epsilon}|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}f_{ixx}(t,e)(x^{1}_{t})^{2}+\delta f_{i}(t,e)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{O}},

\displaystyle A_{2}^{\epsilon}(t)

\displaystyle:=\Big\{\delta g_{x}(t,\Delta^{1},\Delta^{2})\xi_{t}^{1,\epsilon}+\delta g_{y}(t,\Delta^{1},\Delta^{2})\eta_{t}^{1,\epsilon}+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\Big[\delta g_{z^{i}}(t,\Delta^{1},\Delta^{2})(\zeta_{t}^{i,1,\epsilon}-\Delta^{i}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]})

\displaystyle+\delta g_{\tilde{z}^{i}}(t,\Delta^{1},\Delta^{2})\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\lambda_{(t,e)}^{i,1,\epsilon}\nu_{i}(de)\Big]\Big\}\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}+\frac{1}{2}\check{\Xi}(t)\widetilde{D^{2}g}^{\epsilon}(t)\check{\Xi}(t)^{\top}-\frac{1}{2}\tilde{\Xi}(t)D^{2}g(t)\tilde{\Xi}(t)^{\top},

\displaystyle\check{\Xi}(t)

\displaystyle:=\big[\xi_{t}^{1,\epsilon},\eta_{t}^{1,\epsilon},\zeta_{t}^{1,1,\epsilon}-\Delta^{1}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]},\zeta_{t}^{2,1,\epsilon}-\Delta^{2}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]},\int_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\lambda_{(t,e)}^{1,1,\epsilon}\nu_{1}(de),\int_{\mathcal{E}_{2}}\lambda_{(t,e)}^{2,1,\epsilon}\nu_{2}(de)\big].

By the
L^{\beta}
-estimate of FBSDEP in Section 2, we have

\displaystyle E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}(|\xi^{3,\epsilon}_{t}|^{2}+|\eta^{3,\epsilon}_{t}|^{2})+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\bigg[\int_{0}^{T}|\zeta^{i,3,\epsilon}_{t}|^{2}dt+\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|\lambda^{i,3,\epsilon}_{(t,e)}|^{2}\nu_{i}(de)dt\bigg]\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq CE\bigg[\bigg|\frac{1}{2}\widetilde{\phi}_{xx}^{\epsilon}(T)(\xi_{T}^{1,\epsilon})^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\phi_{xx}(\bar{x}_{T})(x_{T}^{1})^{2}\bigg|^{2}+\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}A_{2}^{\epsilon}(t)dt\bigg)^{2}+\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}B^{\epsilon}_{1}(t)dt\bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|C^{\epsilon}_{i1}(t)|^{2}dt\bigg)+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|D^{\epsilon}_{i1}(t,e)|^{2}N_{i}(de,dt)\bigg)\bigg]

(3.24)

\displaystyle\leq CE\bigg[\bigg|\frac{1}{2}\widetilde{\phi}_{xx}^{\epsilon}(T)\big[(\xi_{T}^{1,\epsilon})^{2}-(x_{T}^{1})^{2}\big]+\frac{1}{2}\big[\widetilde{\phi}_{xx}^{\epsilon}(T)-\phi_{xx}(\bar{x}_{T})\big](x_{T}^{1})^{2}\bigg|^{2}+\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}A_{2}^{\epsilon}(t)dt\bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle+\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}B^{\epsilon}_{1}(t)dt\bigg)^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|C^{\epsilon}_{i1}(t)|^{2}dt\bigg)+\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|D^{\epsilon}_{i1}(t,e)|^{2}N_{i}(de,dt)\bigg)\bigg]\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq C\bigg\{E\bigg[\bigg|\frac{1}{2}\widetilde{\phi}_{xx}^{\epsilon}(T)\xi_{T}^{2,\epsilon}(\xi_{T}^{1,\epsilon}+x_{T}^{1})+\frac{1}{2}\big[\widetilde{\phi}_{xx}^{\epsilon}(T)-\phi_{xx}(\bar{x}_{T})\big](x_{T}^{1})^{2}\bigg|^{2}+\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}A_{2}^{\epsilon}(t)dt\bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle+\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}B^{\epsilon}_{1}(t)dt\bigg)^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|C^{\epsilon}_{i1}(t)|^{2}dt\bigg)+\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|D^{\epsilon}_{i1}(t,e)|^{2}N_{i}(de,dt)\bigg)\bigg]\bigg]\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq C\bigg\{E\bigg[|\xi_{T}^{2,\epsilon}|^{2}|\xi_{T}^{1,\epsilon}+x_{T}^{1}|^{2}\bigg]+E\bigg[[\widetilde{\phi}_{xx}^{\epsilon}(T)-\phi_{xx}(\bar{x}_{T})]^{2}(x_{T}^{1})^{4}\bigg]+E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}A_{2}^{\epsilon}(t)dt\bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle+\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}B^{\epsilon}_{1}(t)dt\bigg)^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|C^{\epsilon}_{i1}(t)|^{2}dt\bigg)+\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|D^{\epsilon}_{i1}(t,e)|^{2}N_{i}(de,dt)\bigg)\bigg]\bigg]\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq C\bigg\{\bigg\{E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|\xi_{t}^{2,\epsilon}|^{4}\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bigg\{E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|\xi_{t}^{1,\epsilon}+x_{t}^{1}|^{4}\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\bigg\{E\bigg[[\widetilde{\phi}_{xx}^{\epsilon}(T)-\phi_{xx}(\bar{x}_{T})]^{4}\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bigg\{E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|x_{t}^{1}|^{8}\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}+E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}A_{2}^{\epsilon}(t)dt\bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle+\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}B^{\epsilon}_{1}(t)dt\bigg)^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|C^{\epsilon}_{i1}(t)|^{2}dt\bigg)+\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|D^{\epsilon}_{i1}(t,e)|^{2}N_{i}(de,dt)\bigg)\bigg]\bigg]\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq C\bigg\{o(\epsilon^{2})+E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}A_{2}^{\epsilon}(t)dt\bigg)^{2}+\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}B^{\epsilon}_{1}(t)dt\bigg)^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|C^{\epsilon}_{i1}(t)|^{2}dt\bigg)

\displaystyle+\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|D^{\epsilon}_{i1}(t,e)|^{2}N_{i}(de,dt)\bigg)\bigg]\bigg\}.

Next, we need to give the estimate of
E\big[\big(\int_{0}^{T}A_{2}^{\epsilon}(t)dt\big)^{2}\big]
. In fact,

\displaystyle E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}A_{2}^{\epsilon}(t)dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]=E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\bigg[\delta g_{x}(t,\Delta^{1},\Delta^{2})\xi_{t}^{1,\epsilon}+\delta g_{y}(t,\Delta^{1},\Delta^{2})\eta_{t}^{1,\epsilon}

(3.25)

\displaystyle+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\bigg[\delta g_{z^{i}}(t,\Delta^{1},\Delta^{2})(\zeta_{t}^{i,1,\epsilon}-\Delta^{i}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]})+\delta g_{\tilde{z}^{i}}(t,\Delta^{1},\Delta^{2})\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\lambda_{(t,e)}^{i,1,\epsilon}\nu_{i}(de)\bigg]\bigg]\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\check{\Xi}(t)\widetilde{D^{2}g}^{\epsilon}(t)\check{\Xi}(t)^{\top}-\frac{1}{2}\tilde{\Xi}(t)D^{2}g(t)\tilde{\Xi}(t)^{\top}dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg].

We divide the right hand of (
3.25
) into several parts to get the following estimates:

\displaystyle E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\delta g_{x}(t,\Delta^{1},\Delta^{2})\xi_{t}^{1,\epsilon}\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]\leq CE\bigg[\bigg(\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}\xi_{t}^{1,\epsilon}dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]\leq C\epsilon^{2}E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|\xi_{t}^{1,\epsilon}|^{2}\bigg]\leq o(\epsilon^{2}),

\displaystyle E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\delta g_{z^{i}}(t,\Delta^{1},\Delta^{2})(\zeta_{t}^{i,1,\epsilon}-\Delta^{i}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]})\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq CE\bigg[\bigg(\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}|\delta g_{z^{i}}(t,\Delta^{1},\Delta^{2})||(\zeta_{t}^{i,2,\epsilon}+k^{i}_{1}(t)x_{t}^{1})|dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]\leq CE\bigg[\bigg(\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}|\zeta_{t}^{i,2,\epsilon}+k^{i}_{1}(t)x_{t}^{1}|dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}|\zeta_{t}^{i,2,\epsilon}|^{2}dt\bigg)\bigg]+C\epsilon E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|x_{t}^{1}|^{2}\bigg(\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}|k^{i}_{1}(t)|^{2}dt\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|\zeta_{t}^{i,2,\epsilon}|^{2}dt\bigg)\bigg]+C\epsilon\bigg\{E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|x_{t}^{1}|^{4}\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bigg\{E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}|k^{i}_{1}(t)|^{2}dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq o(\epsilon^{2}),

and similarly,

\displaystyle E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\delta g_{y}(t,\Delta^{1},\Delta^{2})\eta_{t}^{1,\epsilon}\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]\leq o(\epsilon^{2})

\displaystyle E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\delta g_{\tilde{z}^{i}}(t,\Delta^{1},\Delta^{2})\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\lambda_{(t,e)}^{i,1,\epsilon}\nu_{i}(de)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]\leq o(\epsilon^{2}),\ i=1,2.

Then we consider the last part of (
3.25
) as follows. In which, we have

\displaystyle E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\widetilde{g}_{z^{1}z^{1}}^{\epsilon}(t)[\zeta_{t}^{1,1,\epsilon}-\Delta^{1}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}]^{2}-g_{z^{1}z^{1}}(t)[z_{t}^{1,1}-\Delta^{1}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}]^{2}dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\widetilde{g}_{z^{1}z^{1}}^{\epsilon}(t)\big[(\zeta_{t}^{1,1,\epsilon}-\Delta^{1}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]})^{2}-(z_{t}^{1,1}-\Delta^{1}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]})^{2}\big]

\displaystyle+(\widetilde{g}_{z^{1}z^{1}}^{\epsilon}(t)-g_{z^{1}z^{1}}(t))\big[z_{t}^{1,1}-\Delta^{1}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}\big]^{2}dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\widetilde{g}_{z^{1}z^{1}}^{\epsilon}(t)\zeta_{t}^{1,2,\epsilon}\big[\zeta_{t}^{1,2,\epsilon}+2k^{1}_{1}(t)x_{t}^{1}\big]+(\widetilde{g}_{z^{1}z^{1}}^{\epsilon}(t)-g_{z^{1}z^{1}}(t))\big[k^{1}_{1}(t)x_{t}^{1}\big]^{2}dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq CE\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\widetilde{g}_{z^{1}z^{1}}^{\epsilon}(t)\zeta_{t}^{1,2,\epsilon}\big[\zeta_{t}^{1,2,\epsilon}+2k^{1}_{1}(t)x_{t}^{1}\big]dt\bigg)^{2}+\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}(\widetilde{g}_{z^{1}z^{1}}^{\epsilon}(t)-g_{z^{1}z^{1}}(t))\big[k^{1}_{1}(t)x_{t}^{1}\big]^{2}dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq CE\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\zeta_{t}^{1,2,\epsilon}\big[\zeta_{t}^{1,2,\epsilon}+2k^{1}_{1}(t)x_{t}^{1}\big]dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle+E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|x_{t}^{1}|^{4}\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}(\widetilde{g}_{z^{1}z^{1}}^{\epsilon}(t)-g_{z^{1}z^{1}}(t))|k^{1}_{1}(t)|^{2}dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq CE\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|\zeta_{t}^{1,2,\epsilon}|^{2}dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]+CE\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\zeta_{t}^{1,2,\epsilon}k^{1}_{1}(t)x_{t}^{1}dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle+E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|x_{t}^{1}|^{4}\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}(\widetilde{g}_{z^{1}z^{1}}^{\epsilon}(t)-g_{z^{1}z^{1}}(t))|k^{1}_{1}(t)|^{2}dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq o(\epsilon^{2})+CE\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|x_{t}^{1}|^{2}\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\zeta_{t}^{1,2,\epsilon}k^{1}_{1}(t)dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle+E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|x_{t}^{1}|^{4}\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}(\widetilde{g}_{z^{1}z^{1}}^{\epsilon}(t)-g_{z^{1}z^{1}}(t))|k^{1}_{1}(t)|^{2}dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq o(\epsilon^{2})+CE\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|x_{t}^{1}|^{2}\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|\zeta_{t}^{1,2,\epsilon}|^{2}dt\bigg)\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|k^{1}_{1}(t)|^{2}dt\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg\{E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|x_{t}^{1}|^{8}\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bigg\{E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}(\widetilde{g}_{z^{1}z^{1}}^{\epsilon}(t)-g_{z^{1}z^{1}}(t))|k^{1}_{1}(t)|^{2}dt\bigg)^{4}\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq o(\epsilon^{2})+\bigg\{E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|x_{t}^{1}|^{6}\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{3}}\bigg\{E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|\zeta_{t}^{1,2,\epsilon}|^{2}dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bigg\{E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|k^{1}_{1}(t)|^{2}dt\bigg)^{6}\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{6}}

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{2}\bigg\{E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}(\widetilde{g}_{z^{1}z^{1}}^{\epsilon}(t)-g_{z^{1}z^{1}}(t))|k^{1}_{1}(t)|^{2}dt\bigg)^{4}\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq o(\epsilon^{2}),

and similarly, we have

\displaystyle E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\widetilde{g}_{xx}^{\epsilon}(t)|\xi_{t}^{1,\epsilon}|^{2}-g_{xx}(t)|x_{t}^{1}|^{2}dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]\leq o(\epsilon^{2})

\displaystyle E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\widetilde{g}_{yy}^{\epsilon}(t)|\eta_{t}^{1,\epsilon}|^{2}-g_{yy}(t)|y_{t}^{1}|^{2}dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]\leq o(\epsilon^{2})

\displaystyle E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\widetilde{g}_{\tilde{z}^{i}\tilde{z}^{i}}^{\epsilon}(t)\bigg|\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\lambda_{(t,e)}^{i,1,\epsilon}\nu_{i}(de)\bigg|^{2}-g_{\tilde{z}^{i}\tilde{z}^{i}}(t)\bigg|\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,1}\nu_{i}(de)\bigg|^{2}dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]\leq o(\epsilon^{2}),\ i=1,2,

and the other cross terms have the same estimate.

Then, we give the estimates of
E\big[\big(\int_{0}^{T}B_{1}^{\epsilon}(t)dt\big)^{2}\big],E\big[\big(\int_{0}^{T}|C_{i1}^{\epsilon}(t)|^{2}dt\big)\big]
and

E\big(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|D_{i1}^{\epsilon}(t,e)|^{2}N_{i}(de,dt)\big)
, respectively. In fact, we have

\displaystyle E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}B_{1}^{\epsilon}(t)dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]=E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\delta\tilde{b}_{1x}(t)\xi^{1,\epsilon}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}+\frac{1}{2}\tilde{b}^{\epsilon}_{1xx}(t)|\xi^{1,\epsilon}_{t}|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\tilde{b}_{1xx}(t)|x^{1}_{t}|^{2}dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq CE\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\delta\tilde{b}_{1x}(t)\xi^{1,\epsilon}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle+CE\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\frac{1}{2}\tilde{b}^{\epsilon}_{1xx}(t)(|\xi^{1,\epsilon}_{t}|^{2}-|x^{1}_{t}|^{2})+\frac{1}{2}(\tilde{b}^{\epsilon}_{1xx}(t)-\tilde{b}_{1xx}(t))|x^{1}_{t}|^{2}dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq CE\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|\xi^{1,\epsilon}_{t}|^{2}\bigg(\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}\delta\tilde{b}_{1x}(t)dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]+CE\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\frac{1}{2}\tilde{b}^{\epsilon}_{1xx}(t)\xi^{2,\epsilon}_{t}(\xi^{1,\epsilon}_{t}+x^{1}_{t})dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle+CE\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\frac{1}{2}(\tilde{b}^{\epsilon}_{1xx}(t)-\tilde{b}_{1xx}(t))|x^{1}_{t}|^{2}dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{2}E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|\xi^{1,\epsilon}_{t}|^{2}\bigg]+CE\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|\xi^{2,\epsilon}_{t}|^{2}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}(\xi^{1,\epsilon}_{t}+x^{1}_{t})^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle+CE\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|x^{1}_{t}|^{4}\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\frac{1}{2}(\tilde{b}^{\epsilon}_{1xx}(t)-\tilde{b}_{1xx}(t))dt\bigg)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq o(\epsilon^{2})+C\bigg\{E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|\xi^{2,\epsilon}_{t}|^{4}\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bigg\{E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}(\xi^{1,\epsilon}_{t}+x^{1}_{t})^{4}\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\bigg\{E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|x^{1}_{t}|^{8}\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bigg\{E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\frac{1}{2}(\tilde{b}^{\epsilon}_{1xx}(t)-\tilde{b}_{1xx}(t))dt\bigg)^{4}\bigg]\bigg\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq o(\epsilon^{2}),

\displaystyle E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|C_{i1}^{\epsilon}(t)|^{2}dt\bigg)\bigg]=E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|\delta\sigma_{ix}(t)\xi_{t}^{2,\epsilon}\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}+\frac{1}{2}\tilde{\sigma}^{\epsilon}_{ixx}(t)|\xi_{t}^{1,\epsilon}|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\sigma_{ixx}(t)(x^{1}_{t})^{2}|^{2}dt\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq CE\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|\delta\sigma_{ix}(t)|^{2}|\xi_{t}^{2,\epsilon}|^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}+|\frac{1}{2}\tilde{\sigma}^{\epsilon}_{ixx}(t)(|\xi_{t}^{1,\epsilon}|^{2}-|x^{1}_{t}|^{2})+\frac{1}{2}(\tilde{\sigma}^{\epsilon}_{ixx}(t)-\sigma_{ixx}(t))(x^{1}_{t})^{2}|^{2}dt\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq CE\bigg[\int_{\bar{t}}^{\bar{t}+\epsilon}|\delta\sigma_{ix}(t)|^{2}|\xi_{t}^{2,\epsilon}|^{2}dt\bigg]

\displaystyle+CE\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|\frac{1}{2}\tilde{\sigma}^{\epsilon}_{ixx}(t)(|\xi_{t}^{1,\epsilon}|^{2}-|x^{1}_{t}|^{2})|^{2}+|\frac{1}{2}(\tilde{\sigma}^{\epsilon}_{ixx}(t)-\sigma_{ixx}(t))(x^{1}_{t})^{2}|^{2}dt\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|\xi_{t}^{2,\epsilon}|^{2}\bigg]+CE\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|\xi_{t}^{2,\epsilon}|^{2}(\xi_{t}^{1,\epsilon}+x^{1}_{t})^{2}dt\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle+CE\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|x^{1}_{t}|^{4}\int_{0}^{T}(\tilde{\sigma}^{\epsilon}_{ixx}(t)-\sigma_{ixx}(t))^{2}dt\bigg]\leq o(\epsilon^{2}),

and

\displaystyle E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|D_{i1}^{\epsilon}(t,e)|^{2}N_{i}(de,dt)\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle=E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|\delta f_{ix}(t,e)\xi_{t-}^{1,\epsilon}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{O}}+\frac{1}{2}\tilde{f}^{\epsilon}_{ixx}(t,e)|\xi_{t-}^{1,\epsilon}|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}f_{ixx}(t,e)(x^{1}_{t-})^{2}+\delta f_{i}(t,e)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{O}}|^{2}N_{i}(de,dt)\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|\delta f_{ix}(t,e)\xi_{t-}^{1,\epsilon}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{O}}+\frac{1}{2}\tilde{f}^{\epsilon}_{ixx}(t,e)(|\xi_{t-}^{1,\epsilon}|^{2}-|x^{1}_{t-}|^{2})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}(\tilde{f}^{\epsilon}_{ixx}(t,e)-f_{ixx}(t,e))(x^{1}_{t-})^{2}+\delta f_{i}(t,e)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{O}}|^{2}N_{i}(de,dt)\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq E\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|\delta f_{ix}(t,e)|^{2}|\xi_{t-}^{1,\epsilon}|^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{O}}+\Big|\frac{1}{2}\tilde{f}^{\epsilon}_{ixx}(t,e)(|\xi_{t-}^{1,\epsilon}|^{2}-|x^{1}_{t-}|^{2})\Big|^{2}

\displaystyle+\Big|\frac{1}{2}(\tilde{f}^{\epsilon}_{ixx}(t,e)-f_{ixx}(t,e))(x^{1}_{t-})^{2}\Big|^{2}+|\delta f_{i}(t,e)|^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{O}}N_{i}(de,dt)\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq E\bigg[\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[|\xi_{t-}^{2,\epsilon}|^{2}(\xi_{t-}^{1,\epsilon}+x^{1}_{t-})^{2}+(\tilde{f}^{\epsilon}_{ixx}(t,e)-f_{ixx}(t,e))^{2}(x^{1}_{t-})^{4}\bigg|\mathcal{P}\otimes\mathcal{B}(\mathcal{E}_{i})\bigg]\nu_{i}(de)dt\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|\xi_{t}^{2,\epsilon}|^{2}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|\xi_{t}^{1,\epsilon}+x^{1}_{t}|^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle+E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|x^{1}_{t}|^{4}\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[(\tilde{f}^{\epsilon}_{ixx}-f_{ixx})^{2}\bigg|\mathcal{P}\otimes\mathcal{B}(\mathcal{E}_{i})\bigg]\nu_{i}(de)dt\bigg]\leq o(\epsilon^{2}).

Thus, from (
3.24
), the third estimate in (
3.21
) holds. The proof is complete.
∎

Then we give the variation equation for
\tilde{\Gamma}
and its estimates. For simplicity, we rewrite reduced (
2.7
) as follows:

\left\{\begin{aligned} d\tilde{\Gamma}_{t}^{u}&=\tilde{b}_{2}(t,x_{t}^{u},\tilde{\Gamma}_{t}^{u},u_{t})dW^{2}_{t}+\int_{\mathcal{E}_{2}}f_{4}(t,x_{t-}^{u},\tilde{\Gamma}_{t-}^{u},e)\tilde{N}_{2}(de,dt),\quad t\in[0,T],\\
\tilde{\Gamma}_{0}^{u}&=1,\end{aligned}\right.

(3.26)

where

\displaystyle\tilde{b}_{2}(t,x_{t}^{u},\tilde{\Gamma}_{t}^{u},u_{t})

\displaystyle:=\tilde{\Gamma}_{t}^{u}\sigma_{3}^{-1}(t)b_{2}(t,x_{t}^{u},u_{t}),

\displaystyle f_{4}(t,x_{t-}^{u},\tilde{\Gamma}_{t-}^{u},e)

\displaystyle:=\tilde{\Gamma}_{t-}^{u}(\lambda(t,x_{t-}^{u},e)-1).

Similarly, we first have the following results.

Lemma 3.4
.

For the solution
\tilde{\Gamma}
to (
3.26
), we have

E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|\tilde{\Gamma}_{t}^{u}|^{\beta}\bigg]<\infty,\ \ \forall\beta\in\mathbb{R},\ \ u\in\mathcal{U}_{ad}[0,T],

and for any
\beta\geq 2
,

\displaystyle E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|x_{t}^{u}|^{\beta}\bigg]\leq C\Big(1+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}E\big[|u_{t}|^{\beta}\big]\Big),\quad E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|y_{t}^{u}|^{\beta}\bigg]\leq C\Big(1+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}E\big[|u_{t}|^{\beta}\big]\Big),

\displaystyle E\bigg[\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}|z^{i,u}_{t}|^{2}dt\bigg)^{\frac{\beta}{2}}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}|\tilde{z}^{i,u}_{t}|^{2}\nu_{i}(de)dt\bigg)^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\bigg]\leq C\Big(1+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}E\big[|u_{t}|^{\beta}\big]\Big).

From this, the expansions for
\tilde{b}_{2},f_{4}
are given as follows:

\displaystyle\tilde{b}_{2}(t,x^{\epsilon}_{t},\tilde{\Gamma}_{t}^{\epsilon},u^{\epsilon}_{t})-\tilde{b}_{2}(t)

\displaystyle=\tilde{b}_{2x}(t)(x_{t}^{1}+x_{t}^{2})+\tilde{b}_{2\tilde{\Gamma}}(t)(\tilde{\Gamma}_{t}^{1}+\tilde{\Gamma}_{t}^{2})+\frac{1}{2}\big[x_{t}^{1},\tilde{\Gamma}_{t}^{1}\big]D^{2}\tilde{b}_{2}(t)\big[x_{t}^{1},\tilde{\Gamma}_{t}^{1}\big]^{\top}

\displaystyle+\delta\tilde{b}_{2}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}+\delta\tilde{b}_{2x}(t)x^{1}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}+\delta\tilde{b}_{2\tilde{\Gamma}}(t)\tilde{\Gamma}^{1}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}+o(\epsilon),

\displaystyle f_{4}(t,x^{\epsilon}_{t},\tilde{\Gamma}_{t}^{\epsilon},e)-f_{4}(t,e)

\displaystyle=f_{4x}(t,e)(x_{t}^{1}+x_{t}^{2})+f_{4\tilde{\Gamma}}(t,e)(\tilde{\Gamma}_{t}^{1}+\tilde{\Gamma}_{t}^{2})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\big[x_{t}^{1},\tilde{\Gamma}_{t}^{1}\big]D^{2}f_{4}(t,e)\big[x_{t}^{1},\tilde{\Gamma}_{t}^{1}\big]^{\top}+o(\epsilon).

Then we have

\left\{\begin{aligned} d\tilde{\Gamma}^{1}_{t}&=\Big[\tilde{b}_{2x}(t)x_{t}^{1}+\tilde{b}_{2\tilde{\Gamma}}(t)\tilde{\Gamma}_{t}^{1}+\delta\tilde{b}_{2}(t)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}\Big]dW^{2}_{t}+\int_{\mathcal{E}_{2}}\Big[f_{4x}(t,e)x_{t-}^{1}+f_{4\tilde{\Gamma}}(t,e)\tilde{\Gamma}_{t-}^{1}\Big]\tilde{N}_{2}(de,dt),\\
d\tilde{\Gamma}^{2}_{t}&=\Big[\tilde{b}_{2x}(t)x_{t}^{2}+\tilde{b}_{2\tilde{\Gamma}}(t)\tilde{\Gamma}_{t}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\big[x_{t}^{1},\tilde{\Gamma}_{t}^{1}\big]D^{2}\tilde{b}_{2}(t)\big[x_{t}^{1},\tilde{\Gamma}_{t}^{1}\big]^{\top}+\delta\tilde{b}_{2x}(t)x^{1}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}\\
&\qquad+\delta\tilde{b}_{2\tilde{\Gamma}}(t)\tilde{\Gamma}^{1}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}\Big]dW^{2}_{t}+\int_{\mathcal{E}_{2}}\Big[f_{4x}(t,e)x_{t-}^{2}+f_{4\tilde{\Gamma}}(t,e)\tilde{\Gamma}_{t-}^{2}\\
&\qquad+\frac{1}{2}[x_{t-}^{1},\tilde{\Gamma}_{t-}^{1}]D^{2}f_{4}(t,e)[x_{t-}^{1},\tilde{\Gamma}_{t-}^{1}]^{\top}\Big]\tilde{N}_{2}(de,dt),\quad t\in[0,T],\\
\tilde{\Gamma}_{0}^{1}&=0,\ \tilde{\Gamma}_{0}^{2}=0,\end{aligned}\right.

and the following result holds.

Lemma 3.5
.

For
\beta\geq 2
, by
(A1)
, we have

\displaystyle E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|\tilde{\Gamma}^{1}_{t}|^{\beta}\bigg]=O(\epsilon^{\frac{\beta}{2}}),\quad E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|\tilde{\Gamma}^{2}_{t}|^{\beta}\bigg]=O(\epsilon^{\beta}),

\displaystyle E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|\tilde{\Gamma}^{\epsilon}_{t}-\bar{\tilde{\Gamma}}_{t}-\tilde{\Gamma}^{1}_{t}|^{2}\bigg]=O(\epsilon^{2}),\quad E\bigg[\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|\tilde{\Gamma}^{\epsilon}_{t}-\bar{\tilde{\Gamma}}_{t}-\tilde{\Gamma}^{1}_{t}-\tilde{\Gamma}^{2}_{t}|^{2}\bigg]=o(\epsilon^{2}).

3.4
Adjoint equations and the maximum principle

Let
\tilde{l}(t,\Theta^{u}(t),\tilde{\Gamma}^{u}_{t},u_{t}):=\tilde{\Gamma}^{u}_{t}l(t,\Theta^{u}(t),u_{t}),\ \tilde{\Phi}(x_{T}^{u},\tilde{\Gamma}^{u}_{T}):=\tilde{\Gamma}^{u}_{T}\Phi(x_{T}^{u})
,
and the cost functional (
3.2
) can be rewritten as

J(u)=E\bigg\{\int_{0}^{T}\tilde{l}\big(t,\Theta^{u}(t),\tilde{\Gamma}^{u}_{t},u_{t}\big)dt+\tilde{\Phi}(x_{T}^{u},\tilde{\Gamma}^{u}_{T})+\Gamma(y_{0}^{u})\bigg\}.

(3.27)

Therefore, the expansion of
\tilde{l},\tilde{\Phi}
and
\Gamma
can be given as:

\displaystyle\tilde{l}\big(t,x^{\epsilon}_{t},y^{\epsilon}_{t},z^{1,\epsilon}_{t},z^{2,\epsilon}_{t},\tilde{z}^{1,\epsilon}_{(t,e)},\tilde{z}^{2,\epsilon}_{(t,e)},\tilde{\Gamma}_{t}^{\epsilon},u^{\epsilon}_{t}\big)-\tilde{l}(t)

\displaystyle=\tilde{l}_{x}(t)(x_{t}^{1}+x_{t}^{2})+\tilde{l}_{y}(t)(y_{t}^{1}+y_{t}^{2})+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg[\tilde{l}_{z^{i}}(t)(z_{t}^{i,1^{\prime}}+z_{t}^{i,2})+\tilde{l}_{\tilde{z}^{i}}(t)\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}(\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,1}+\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,2})\nu_{i}(de)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\chi(t)D^{2}\tilde{l}(t)\chi(t)^{\top}+\tilde{l}_{\tilde{\Gamma}}(t)(\tilde{\Gamma}_{t}^{1}+\tilde{\Gamma}_{t}^{2})+\delta\tilde{l}(t,\Delta^{1},\Delta^{2})\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}+o(\epsilon),

\displaystyle\tilde{\Phi}(x_{T}^{\epsilon},\tilde{\Gamma}^{\epsilon}_{T})-\tilde{\Phi}(\bar{x}_{T},\bar{\tilde{\Gamma}}_{T})

\displaystyle=\tilde{\Phi}_{x}(\bar{x}_{T},\bar{\tilde{\Gamma}}_{T})(x^{1}_{T}+x^{2}_{T})+\tilde{\Phi}_{\tilde{\Gamma}}(\bar{x}_{T},\bar{\tilde{\Gamma}}_{T})(\tilde{\Gamma}_{T}^{1}+\tilde{\Gamma}_{T}^{2})+\frac{1}{2}\big[x^{1}_{T},\tilde{\Gamma}_{T}^{1}\big]D^{2}\tilde{\Phi}(T)\big[x^{1}_{T},\tilde{\Gamma}_{T}^{1}\big]^{\top}+o(\epsilon),

\displaystyle\Gamma(y^{\epsilon}_{0})-\Gamma(\bar{y}_{0})=\Gamma_{y}(\bar{y}_{0})(y^{1}_{0}+y^{2}_{0})+\frac{1}{2}\Gamma_{yy}(\bar{y}_{0})(y^{1}_{0})^{2}+o(\epsilon),

where
\chi(t)=\big[x_{t}^{1},y_{t}^{1},z_{t}^{1,1^{\prime}},z_{t}^{2,1^{\prime}},\int_{\mathcal{E}_{1}}\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{1,1}\nu_{1}(de),\int_{\mathcal{E}_{2}}\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{2,1}\nu_{2}(de),\tilde{\Gamma}_{t}^{1}\big]
. Then the expansion of cost functional (
3.27
) is given by

\displaystyle J(u^{\epsilon})-J(\bar{u})=E\bigg\{\int_{0}^{T}\bigg[\tilde{l}_{x}(t)(x_{t}^{1}+x_{t}^{2})+\tilde{l}_{\tilde{\Gamma}}(t)(\tilde{\Gamma}_{t}^{1}+\tilde{\Gamma}_{t}^{2})+\tilde{l}_{y}(t)(y_{t}^{1}+y_{t}^{2})

(3.28)

\displaystyle+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\tilde{l}_{z^{i}}(t)(z_{t}^{i,1^{\prime}}+z_{t}^{i,2})+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\tilde{l}_{\tilde{z}^{i}}(t)\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}(\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,1}+\tilde{z}_{(t,e)}^{i,2})\nu_{i}(de)+\frac{1}{2}\chi(t)D^{2}\tilde{l}(t)\chi(t)^{\top}

\displaystyle+\delta\tilde{l}(t,\Delta^{1},\Delta^{2})\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}\bigg]dt+\tilde{\Phi}_{x}(\bar{x}_{T},\bar{\tilde{\Gamma}}_{T})(x^{1}_{T}+x^{2}_{T})+\tilde{\Phi}_{\tilde{\Gamma}}(\bar{x}_{T},\bar{\tilde{\Gamma}}_{T})(\tilde{\Gamma}_{T}^{1}+\tilde{\Gamma}_{T}^{2})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\big[x^{1}_{T},\tilde{\Gamma}_{T}^{1}\big]D^{2}\tilde{\Phi}(T)\big[x^{1}_{T},\tilde{\Gamma}_{T}^{1}\big]^{\top}+\Gamma_{y}(\bar{y}_{0})(y^{1}_{0}+y^{2}_{0})+\frac{1}{2}\Gamma_{yy}(\bar{y}_{0})(y^{1}_{0})^{2}\bigg\}+o(\epsilon).

Then we introduce the new adjoint equations for
x^{1}+x^{2},y^{1}+y^{2},z^{1,1}+z^{1,2},z^{2,1}+z^{2,2},\tilde{z}^{1,1}+\tilde{z}^{1,2},\tilde{z}^{2,1}+\tilde{z}^{2,2}
and
\tilde{\Gamma}^{1}+\tilde{\Gamma}^{2}
:

\left\{\begin{aligned} dh_{t}&=\big[\tilde{l}_{y}(t)+h_{t}g_{y}(t)\big]dt+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\big[\tilde{l}_{z^{i}}(t)+h_{t}g_{z^{i}}(t)\big]dW^{i}_{t}\\
&\quad+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\big[\tilde{l}_{\tilde{z}^{i}}(t)+h_{t-}g_{\tilde{z}^{i}}(t)\big]\tilde{N}_{i}(de,dt),\\
-dr_{t}&=\bigg[\tilde{l}_{\tilde{\Gamma}}(t)+s^{2}_{t}\tilde{b}_{2\tilde{\Gamma}}+\int_{\mathcal{E}_{2}}\mathbb{E}_{2}\big[f_{4\tilde{\Gamma}}(t,e)|\mathcal{P}\otimes\mathcal{B}({\mathcal{E}_{2}})\big]\tilde{s}^{2}_{(t,e)}\nu_{2}(de)\bigg]dt\\
&\quad-\sum^{2}_{i=1}s^{i}_{t}dW^{i}_{t}-\sum^{2}_{i=1}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\tilde{s}^{i}_{(t,e)}\tilde{N}_{i}(de,dt),\\
h_{0}&=\Gamma_{y}(\bar{y}_{0}),\quad r_{T}=\tilde{\Phi}_{\tilde{\Gamma}}(\bar{x}_{T},\bar{\tilde{\Gamma}}_{T}),\end{aligned}\right.

(3.29)

\left\{\begin{aligned} -dm_{t}&=\bigg[\tilde{l}_{x}(t)+h_{t}g_{x}(t)+m_{t}\tilde{b}_{1x}(t)+\sum^{2}_{i=1}n^{i}_{t}\sigma_{ix}(t)+s^{2}_{t}\tilde{b}_{2x}(t)\\
&\qquad+\sum^{2}_{i=1}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\mathbb{E}_{i}\big[f_{ix}(t,e)|\mathcal{P}\otimes\mathcal{B}({\mathcal{E}_{i}})\big]\tilde{n}^{i}_{(t,e)}\nu_{i}(de)\\
&\qquad+\int_{\mathcal{E}_{2}}\mathbb{E}_{2}\big[f_{4x}(t,e)|\mathcal{P}\otimes\mathcal{B}({\mathcal{E}_{2}})\big]\tilde{s}^{2}_{(t,e)}\nu_{2}(de)\bigg]dt\\
&\quad-\sum^{2}_{i=1}n^{i}_{t}dW^{i}_{t}-\sum^{2}_{i=1}\int_{\mathcal{E}_{i}}\tilde{n}^{i}_{(t,e)}\tilde{N}_{i}(de,dt),\quad t\in[0,T]\\
m_{T}&=\tilde{\Phi}_{x}(\bar{x}_{T},\bar{\tilde{\Gamma}}_{T})+h_{T}\phi_{x}(\bar{x}_{T}),\end{aligned}\right.

(3.30)

with unique solution
(h,m,n^{1},n^{2},\tilde{n}^{1},\tilde{n}^{2},r,s^{1},s^{2},\tilde{s}^{1},\tilde{s}^{2})\in\mathcal{M}^{\beta}[0,T]\times\mathcal{N}^{\beta}[0,T]
. Applying Itô’s formula to
m_{t}(x^{1}_{t}+x^{2}_{t})-h_{t}(y_{t}^{1}+y_{t}^{2})+r_{t}(\tilde{\Gamma}_{t}^{1}+\tilde{\Gamma}_{t}^{2})
, and by (
3.28
), we have

\displaystyle J(u^{\epsilon})-J(\bar{u})=E\bigg\{\int_{0}^{T}\bigg[\Big(-\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg[\tilde{l}_{z^{i}}p_{t}\delta\sigma_{i}(t)+h_{t}g_{z^{i}}p_{t}\delta\sigma_{i}(t)-n^{i}_{t}\delta\sigma_{i}(t)\bigg]

(3.31)

\displaystyle+h_{t}\delta g(t,\Delta^{1},\Delta^{2})+\delta\tilde{l}(t,\Delta^{1},\Delta^{2})+m_{t}\delta\tilde{b}_{1}+s^{2}_{t}\delta\tilde{b}_{2}\Big)\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}+\frac{1}{2}m_{t}\tilde{b}_{1xx}(x^{1}_{t})^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigg[\frac{1}{2}n^{i}_{t}\sigma_{ixx}(x^{1}_{t})^{2}+n^{i}_{t}\delta\sigma_{ix}x^{1}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{[\bar{t},\bar{t}+\epsilon]}\bigg]+\frac{1}{2}s^{2}_{t}[x^{1}_{t},\tilde{\Gamma}^{1}_{t}]D^{2}\tilde{b}_{2}(t)[x^{1}_{t},\tilde{\Gamma}^{1}_{t}]^{\top}
